*hari hari*—O Lord Hari; *biphale—*unsuccessfully; *janama—*human form of life; *goñāinu*—spend; *manuṣya-janama—*human birth; *pāiya—*having received; *rādhā-kṛṣṇa—*Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *nā*—not; *bhajiyā*—worshipping; *jāniyā—*knowingly; *śuniyā—*having heard; *biṣa—*poison; *khāinu—*drank; *golokera—*of Goloka; *prema-dhana—*the treasure of love; *hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana—*the performance of congregational chanting of the holy names of Hari; *rati—*attachment; *nā—*not; *janmilo—*develop; *kena—*why; *tāya—*in that; *saṁsāra—*material existence; *biṣānale*—in the fire of poison; *dibā-niśi—*day and night; *hiyā—*heart; *jvale*—burns; *juḍāite*—to cool down; *nā*—not; *koinu—*do; *upāya*—any endeavour; *brajendra-nandana—*the son of the King of Braja; *jei—*He who; *śacī-suta—*son of Śacī; *haila—*became; *sei—*He; *balarāma—*Lord Balarāma; *haila—*became; *nitāi—*Lord Nityānanda; *dīna—*poor; *hīna*—low born; *jata chila—*all that were; *harināme—*through the chanting of holy names of Hari; *uddhārilo—*delivered; *tāra sākṣi—*evidence of this; *jagāi madhāi*—Jagāi and Madhāi; *hā hā prabhu*—O my Lord; *nanda-suta*—the son of Nanda Mahārāja; *vṛṣabhānu-sutā—*the daughter of King Vṛṣabhanu; *juta—*along with; *karunā*—mercy; *karoho—*be; *ei-bāro—*this time; *narottama-dāsa—*Narottama dāsa ; *kaya—*says; *nā—*not; *ṭheliho—*push; *rāṅgā pāya—*reddened lotus feet; *tomā bine—*without You; *ke āche—*who else is there; *āmāra*—for me.

**Комментарий:**

This is a song sung by Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura, a very stalwart *ācārya* in the line of Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *sampradāya*, disciplic succession. He has sung many songs, important songs, and his songs are accepted as Vedic conclusion. Very authoritative songs. So he says, praying to Lord Kṛṣṇa, “My dear Lord,” *hari hari*, “I have simply spoiled my life”. *Hari hari biphale janama goñāinu.* Why have you spoiled your life? He says, *manuṣya-janama pāiyā,* “I got this human form of life,” *rādhā-kṛṣṇa nā bhajiyā,* “but I did not care to worship Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. Therefore I have spoiled my life.” And how it is? It is exactly like one who takes poison knowingly. If somebody takes poison unknowingly, there is excuse, but if somebody takes poison knowingly, it is suicidal. So he says, “I have committed suicide simply by not worshipping Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in this human form of life.”

Then he says*, golokera prema-dhana, hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana*. This Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, *saṅkīrtana* movement, is nothing material. It is directly imported from the spiritual kingdom known as Goloka Vṛndāvana. So *golokera prema-dhana*. It is not an ordinary song. It is just the treasure of love of Godhead. So, “But I have no attraction for this.” *Rati nā janmilo kene tāy*. “I have no attraction for it. On the contrary,” *viṣaya-bisānale, dibā-niśi hiyā jwale, “*and because I did not accept it, therefore the blazing fire of the poison of material existence is constantly burning me.” *Dibā-niśi hiyā jwale*. “Day and night, my heart is burning on account of this poisonous effect of material existence.” *taribare nā koinu upāy*. “But I did not seek any remedy for this.” In other words, the remedy for this blazing fire of material existence is this *saṅkīrtana* movement. It is imported from the spiritual kingdom. And who has imported it? Or who has brought it?

Then he says, *brajendra-nandana jei, śaci-suta hoilo sei.* Brajendra-nandana, the son of King of Braja. That is Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa is known as the son of Nanda Mahārāja. He was King of Brajabhūmi. So *brajendra-nandana jei*, the same personality who was formerly the son of Nanda Mahārāja has now appeared as the son of Mother Śacī. *Śacī-suta hoilo sei.* And *balarāma hoilo nitāi*, and Lord Balarāma has become Nityānanda. So by these two brothers adventing, They are delivering all kinds of fallen souls. *Papī-tāpī jata chilo*. As many fallen souls there were within this world, They are delivering them simply by this chanting process. *Harināme uddhārilo,* simply by this chanting. How it is possible? Then he says*, tāra sākṣī jagāi madhāi*. The living example is the two brothers, Jagāi and Mādhāi. These two brothers Jagāi and Madhāi, happened to be born in a *brāhmaṇa* family but they turned out to be debauchees number one. And of course, nowadays, in this age, their qualification is not considered debauchee. Their debauchery was because they were drunkards and woman-hunters. Therefore they were called debauchee. And meat-eaters also. But later on they became delivered by Lord Caitanya and Nityānanda.

So Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura’s explanation says that in this age although people are drunkards, woman-hunters, meat-eaters, and gamblers, all kinds of sinful actors, still, if they take to this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, they will be delivered, undoubtedly. This is the blessings of Lord Caitanya. Then Narottama dāsa Ṭhakura prays, *hā hā prabhu nanda-suta, vṛṣabhānu-sutā-juta*. “My dear Lord Kṛṣṇa, You are son of King Nanda, and Your consort Rādhārāṇī is the daughter of King Vṛṣabhānu. So You are standing together here.” *Narottama dāsa kahe, nā ṭheliho rāṅgā pāya*, “Now I am surrendering unto You, please do not kick me away or push me away with Your lotus feet because I have no other shelter. I am just taking shelter unto Your lotus feet without having any other means. So please accept me and deliver me.” This is the sum and substance of this song.

## Song 5—prāṇeśvara! nivedana ei jana kare

*prāṇeśvara! nivedana ei jana kare*  
*govinda gokulacandra parama ānanda-kanda*  
*gopī-kūla priya dekha more*  
*tuyā priya pada-seva ei dhana more dibā*  
*tumi prabhu karuṇāra nidhi*  
*parama maṅgala yaśa śravaṇe parama rasa*  
*kāra kibā kārjya nahe siddhi*  
*dāruṇa-saṁsāra-gati viṣayete lubdha-mati*  
*tuyā vismaraṇa-śela buke*  
*jara jara tanū-mana acetana anukṣaṇa*  
*jīyante marana bhela duḥkhe*  
*mo-baḍa adhama jane kara kṛpā nirīkṣaṇe*  
*dāsa kari rākha vṛndābane*  
*śri-kṛṣṇa caitanya nāma prabhu mora gaura-dhāma*  
*narottama laila śaraṇe*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prāṇeśvara*—Lord of my life; *nivedana—*request; *ei jana—*this person; *kare—*does; *govinda—*Lord Govinda; *gokulacandra—*moon of Gokula; *parama—*supreme; *ānanda-kanda—*source of happiness; *gopī-kula—*to the gopīs; *priya—*dear; *dekha more—*please glance on me; *tuyā—*Your; *priya—*dea*r; pada-seva—*service of the lotus feet; *ei dhana—*this treasure; *more dibā—*please give me; *tumi—*You; *prabhu—*the Lord; *karuṇāra—*of mercy; *nidhi—*ocea*n; parama—*great; *maṅgala—*auspicious; *jaśa—*fame; *śravaṇe—*by hea*ring; parama rasa—*transcendental mellow; *kāra—*whose; *kibā—*what; *kārjya—*purpose; *nahe—*is not; *siddhi—*successful; *dāruṇa—*formidable; *saṁsāra-gati—*material existence; *viṣayete—*in mundane enjoyments; *lubdha—*attracted; *mati*—the mind; *tuyā—*Your; *vismaraṇa—*forgetfulness; *śela—*pain; *buke—*in the heart; *jara jara—*inert; *tanū—*the body; *mana—*the mind; *acetana—*unconscious; *anukṣaṇa—*always; *jīyante—*while living; *maraṇa—*dead; *bhela—*became; *dūḥkhe—*with distress; *mo-baḍa—*more than me; *adhama jane—*very fallen soul; *kara kṛpā—*be merciful; *nirikṣaṇe—*glance; *dāsa-kari—*as a servant; *rākha—*keep; *vṛndāvane—*in Vṛndāvana; *śri-kṛṣṇa-caitanya-nāma—*the holy names of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya; *prabhu—*Lord; *mora—*mine; *gaura-dhāma—*golden complexion; *narottama—*Narottama dāsa; *laila—*took; *śaraṇe—*shelter.

**Перевод:**

**O Govinda, Lord of my life, You are the moon of Gokula, the abode of transcendental bliss and the beloved of the gopīs. Please cast Your glance my way.**

**O my Lord, since You are an ocean of mercy, I pray for a drop of that mercy—the treasure of serving Your lotus feet. Even hearing Your glories is supremely auspicious and bestows transcendental bliss on the hearer and fulfills all desires.**

**Due to forgetfulness of You I wander in this terrible material life, attached to sense gratification and my mind and body shattered and spiritually unconscious. Like this I am like a dying man in pain and misery.**

**Narottama dāsa prays to his golden complexioned Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, “Please give Your merciful glance on me and keep me as Your servant in Vṛndāvana. You are my only shelter.”**

## Song 6—hari hari! kṛpā kari rākha nija-pade

*hari hari! kṛpā kari rākha nija-pade*  
*kāma krodha chaya jane lañā phire nānā sthāne*  
*viṣaya-bhuṅjāya nānā-mate*  
*haiyā māyāra dāsa kari nānā abhilāṣa*  
*tomāra smaraṇa gela dūre*  
*artha-lābha ei āśe kapaṭa-vaiṣṇava-veśe*  
*bhramiyā bulaye ghare ghare*  
*aneka duḥkhera pare layechile braja-pūre*  
*kṛpā-ḍora galāya baṅdhiyā*  
*daiva-māyā balāt kāre khasāiya sei ḍore*  
*bhava kūpe dileka ḍariya*  
*punaḥ yadi kṛpā-kari e janāre keśa dhari*  
*tāniyā tulaha braja-dhāme*  
*tabe se dekhiya bhāla natubā parāṇa gela*  
*kahe dīna dāsa narottama*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*—O Lord Hari; *kṛpā kari—*be merciful; *rākha—*keep; *nija-pade—*at Your lotus feet; *kāma—*lust; *krodha—*anger; *chaya jane—*six enemies; *lañā—*taking me; *phire—*wander; *nānā sthāne—*in various places; *viṣaya—*sense objects; *bhuṅjāya—*make me enjoy; *nānā-mate—*in different ways; *haiyā—*being; *māyāra dāsa—*a servant of māyā; *kari—*do; *nānā—*various; *abhilāṣa—*desires; *tomāra—*Your; *smaraṇa—*remember; *gela-dure—*went away; *artha-lābha—*accumulating wealth; *ei āśe—*with this desire; *kapaṭa—*pseudo; *baiṣṇava-veśe—*guise of a vaiṣṇava; *bhramiyā-bulaye—*wander; *ghare ghare—*house to house; *aneka*—many; *duḥkhera—*of miseries; *pare—*after; *layechile—*took; *braja-pure—*in Braja; *kṛpā—*compassion; *dora—*ropes; *galāya—*in the neck; *baṅdhiyā—*tied; *daiva*—*māyā—*by external potency; *balāt kāre—*force; *khasāiya*—untied; *sei ḍore—*that rope; *bhava-kūpe—*in the well of material existence; *dileka ḍariya*—threw; *punaḥ*—again; *yadi—*if; *kṛpā-kari—*be merciful; *e janāre—*this person; *keśa dhari—*grabbing the hair; *tāniyā tulaha—*pull me up; *braja-dhāme—*in the abode of Vṛndāvana; *tabe se—*then; *dekhiya bhāla—*it is auspicious; *natuba—*otherwise; *parāṅa gela—*I will die; *kahe—*says; *dīna—*poor; *dāsa narottama—*Narottama dāsa .

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, please be merciful and keep me safe at Your lotus feet. The six enemies headed by lust and anger are dragging me to different places enticing me to enjoy my senses in various ways.**

**Forgetting You, I became a servant of Māyā, and desired so many things for myself. Then, hoping to collect some money, I disguised myself as a Vaiṣṇava and wandered from door to door.**

**After a great deal of suffering, You then kindly tied Your rope of mercy around my neck and brought me back to Vṛndāvana. But then Your external potency Māyādevī forcibly untied that rope and threw me into the well of material existence.**

**So if you kindly once again catch my hair and pull me out of the well and place me in Braja-dhāma, only then will I be saved. Thus prays poor Narottama dāsa.**

## Song 7—hari hari! baḍa śela marame rahila

*hari hari! baḍa śela marame rahila*  
*pāiyā dūrlabha tanu śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhajana vinū*  
*janma mora biphala haila*  
*brajendranandana hari nabadvīpe avatāri*  
*jagata bhariyā prema dila*  
*muñi se pāmara-mati biśeṣe kaṭhina ati*  
*teṅhi more karuṇā nahila*  
*svarūpa sanātana rūpa raghunātha bhaṭṭa-yuga*  
*tāhāte nā haila mora mati*  
*divya cintāmaṇi dhāma vṛndāvana hena sthāna*  
*sei dhāme nā kainū vasati*  
*viśeṣa viṣaye mati nahila vaiṣṇave rati*  
*nirantara kheda uṭhe mane*  
*narottama dāsa kahe jīvera ucita nahe*  
*śrī-guru-vaiṣṇava-sevā vine*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*—O Lord Hari; *baḍa*—great; *śela—*javelin; *marame*—in the heart; *rahila—*remained; *pāiya—*having received; *durlabha—*rare birth; *tanu—*human body; *śrī-kṛṣṇa—*Lord Kṛṣṇa; *bhajana vinu—*without worshipping; *janma—*human form of life; *mora—*my; *biphala haila—*became useless; *brajendranandana—*the son of the King of Braja; *hari—*Lord Hari; *nabadvīpe*—in Navadvīpa; *avatāri—*by incarnating; *jagata—*the world; *bhariyā—*filled; *prema dila*—distributing love of God; *muñi*—I am; *se pāmara-mati—*that sinful-minded; *viśeṣe*—particularly; *kaṭhina ati—*hard; *teṅhi—*that is why; *more—*to me; *karuṇā—*mercy; *nahila—*did not bestow; *svarūpa*—Śrīla Svarūpa Dāmodara Gosvāmī; *sanātana—*Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī; *rūpa—*Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī; *raghunātha—*Śrīla Raghunātha dāsa Gosvāmī; *bhaṭṭa-yuga—*Śrīla Gopāla Bhaṭṭa Gosvāmī and Śrīla Raghunātha Bhaṭṭa Gosvāmī; *tāhāte—*in them; *nā*—did not; *kaila mora mati—*pay attention; *divya—*transcendental; *cintāmaṇi dhāma—*the abode with spiritual gems; *vṛndāvana—*Śrī Vṛndāvana; *hena sthāna—*such a place; *sei dhāme—*in that abode; *nā kainū—*did not; *vasati—*reside; *viśeṣa*—particularly; *viṣaye—*material sense objects; *mati—*attached to; *nahila—*did not develop; *vaiṣṇave—*in Vaiṣṇava; *rati—*attachment; *nirantara—*constantly; *kheda uṭhe—*lament; *mane—*in my mind; *narottama dāsa—*Narottama dāsa; *kahe—*says; *jīvera—*of the living entities; *ucita nahe—*improper; *śrī-guru—*the spiritual master; *vaiṣṇava—*the Vaiṣṇavas; *sevā*—service; *vine—*without.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, a great javeling of lamentation is piercing my heart. Even after attaining this rare human body, still I have not worshipped my beloved Śrī Kṛṣṇa and thus my life has become useless.**

**Lord Hari, though the son of the King of Vraja appeared in Navadvīpa, and filled the whole world with love of God, but I alone could not receive His mercy because I am so sinful and hard-hearted.**

**My mind refused to be attracted to Śrīla Svarūpa Dāmodara Gosvāmī, Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī, Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, Śrīla Raghunātha dāsa Gosvāmī, Śrīla Gopāla Bhaṭṭa Gosvāmī and Śrīla Raghunātha Bhaṭṭa Gosvāmī, nor did I desire to reside in Vṛndāvana.**

**My mind is totally absorbed in sense enjoyment and I have not developed attachment for the Vaiṣṇavas. Always suffering and lamenting, Narottama dāsa implores you to not live without serving Śrī Guru and the Vaiṣṇavas.**

## Song 8—hari hari! ki mora karama abhāga

*hari hari! ki mora karama abhāga*  
*biphale jīvana gela hṛdaye rahila śela*  
*nāhi bhela hari-anurāga*  
*yajña dāna tīrtha-sthāna puṇya-karma japa dhyāna*  
*akāraṇe saba gela mohe*  
*bujhilāma mane hena upahāsa haya jena*  
*vastra hīna alaṅkāra dehe*  
*sādhu-mukhe kathāmṛta śuniyā bimala citta*  
*nāhi bhela aparādha-karāṇa*  
*satata asat-saṅga sakali haila bhaṅga*  
*ki kariba āile śamana*  
*śruti smṛti sadā rabe śuniyāchi ei sabe*  
*hari-pada abhaya śaraṇa*  
*janama laiyā sūkhe kṛṣṇa nā balinū mūkhe*  
*nā karinū se rūpa-bhāvana*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa dūṅha-pāya tanū mana rahu tāya*  
*āra dūre jāuka bāsanā*  
*narottama dāse kaya āra mora nāhi bhaya*  
*tanū mana saṁpinū āpanā*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*—O Lord Hari; *ki—*alas; *mora—*mine; *karama—*fruitive activities; *abhāga—*unfortunate; *biphale—*useless; *jīvana—*human life; *gela—*spend; *hṛdaye—*in the heart; *rahila—*remained; *śela—*pain; *nāhi bhela—*did not have; *hari—*Lord Hari; *anurāga*—attachment; *yajña—*sacrifices; *dāna—*giving charity; *tīrtha-sthāna*—travelling to the holy places of pilgrimage; *pūṇya-karma—*pious activities; *japa—*chanting mantras; *dhyāna*—meditation; *akāraṇe—*wasted; *saba—*all; *gela mohe—*in illusion; *bujhilāma—*understood; *mane—*in the mind; *hena—*that; *upahāsa—*jokes; *haya jena—*like; *vastra-hīna—*without clothes; *alaṅkara—*ornaments; *dehe—*in the body; *sādhu-mukhe—*through the mouth of the devotees; *kathāmṛta—*nectarean topics; *śuniyā—*hearing; *bimala—*purified; *cita—*heart; *nāhi bhela—*did not take place; *aparādha-karāṇa—*due to the offences; *satata—*always; *asat-saṅga*—bad association; *sakali—*everything; *haila bhaṅga—*is lost; *ki kariba—*what will I do; *āile—*if came; *śamana—*the Lord of death; *śruti—*śrutis; *smṛti—*the smṛtis; *sadā—*always; *rabe—*declare; *śuniyāchi—*I have heard; *ei sabe—*all these; *hari-pada—*the lotus feet of Hari; *abhaya*—fearless; *śaraṇa—*shelter; *janama—*taking birth; *laiyā sukhe—*happily; *kṛṣṇa—*the holy names of Kṛṣṇa; *nā balinū—*did not chant; *mukhe—*with the mouth; *nā karinū—*did not; *se rūpa—*that form; *bhāvana—*meditate; *rādhā-kṛṣṇa*—Śrī Rādhikā and Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *duṅha—*both; *pāya*—lotus feet; *tanu—*the body; *mana—*the mind; *rahu—*remain; *tāya—*in them *āra—*and; *dūre jāuka—*go away; *bāsanā—*desires; *narottama dāse*—Narottama dāsa; *kaya—*says; *āra mora nāhi—*I have no more; *bhaya—*fear; *tanū—*the body; *mana—*the mind; *saṁpinū*—I have surrendered; *āpanā—*mine.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari! Alas! My own misdeeds have made me an unfortunate wretch. There is an intense pain of having wasted my life by not developing love for You, which remains and grows in my heart.**

**My Sacrifice, charity, bathing at holy places, pious activities, chanting mantras and meditation are all useless and like an illusion. These religious activities are just jokes, or like ornaments on a naked body.**

**Even after hearing the nectarean topics of Kṛṣṇa, my mind is still not purified because of my offenses. I always associate with non-devotees, and thus break all moral principles of life. What will I do when death comes to me?**

**I have heard that the śrūtis and śmṛtis declared that we should take shelter of the lotus feet of Lord Hari which grant fearlessness, but still I never chanted the holy name of Lord Kṛṣṇa or meditated on His beautiful form.**

**Now I will fix my mind and body at the lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and my material desires will be vanquished. Narottama dāsa says, “Since I have now surrendered unto You, my Lord, I no longer have any fears.”**

## Song 9—Svabhiṣṭa-lālasā (Intense Hankering)

*hari hari! hena dina haibe āmāra*  
*duṅhū aṅga paraśiba duṅhu aṅga nirakhiba*  
*sevana kariba doṅhākāra*  
*lalitā-viśākhā-saṅge sevana kariba raṅge*  
*mālā gaṅthi' diba nānā phūle*  
*kanaka-sampūṭa kari karpūra tāmbūla bhari*  
*yogaiba adhara-jugale*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa śri-caraṇa sei mora prāṇa-dhana*  
*sei mora jīvana-upāya*  
*jaya patita-pāvana deha more ei dhana*  
*tūyā-vine anya nāhi bhāya*  
*śrī-guru karuṇā-sindhu adhama-janāra bandhu*  
*lokanātha lokera jīvana*  
*hā hā prabhū! kara daya deha more pada-chāyā*  
*narottama laila śaraṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*—O Lord Hari; *hena—*such; *dina*—day; *haibe*—will be; *āmāra*—mine; *duṅhū—*both; *aṅga*—limb; *paraśiba*—touch; *duṅhu—*both; *aṅga—*limbs; *nirakhiba—*behold; *sebana kariba—*serve; *doṅhākāra—*both of you; *lalitā—*Śrī Lalitā; *viśākhā—*Śrī Viśākhā; *saṅge—*in the association of; *sevana kariba raṅge*—will serve happily; *mālā gaṅthi—*make flower garlands; *diba—*will offer; *nānā phule—*various flowers; *kanaka—*golden; *samputa kari—*box; *karpura—*camphor; *tāmbūla*—betel nuts; *puri—*fill up; *yogaiba—*supply; *adhara-jugale—*in the mouth; *rādhā-kṛṣṇa*—Śrī Rādhikā and Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *śrī-caraṇa*—lotus feet; *sei—*this; *mora—*mine; *prāṇa-dhana—*life and soul; s*ei mora—*this is my; *jīvana-upāya—*survival of life; *jaya*—all glories; *patita-pāvana—*the deliverer of the fallen souls; *deha—*please give; *more—*to me; *ei dhana—*this wealth; *tuyā-vine—*without you; *anya nāhi—*no one else; *bhāya—*acceptable; *śrī-guru*—the spiritual master; *karuṇā-sindhu—*ocean of mercy; *adhama-janāra—*of the fallen souls; *bandhu—*friend; *lokanātha*—Śrīla Lokanātha Gosvāmī; *lokera jībana—*the life of the people; *hā hā prabhu!—*O my Lord; *kara daya—*be merciful; *deha more—*please give me; *pada-chāyā—*shelter of your lotus feet; *narottama—*Narottama dāsa; *laila—*take; *śaraṇa—*shelter.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, when will that day be mine when I can behold Śrī Rādhīkā beside You, and I will touch Your lotus feet and serve You both together?**

**When will that day come when I can happily serve You both, along with Lalitā and Viśākhā, making garlands for You and offering You tāmbula, camphor, and betelnuts in a golden box?**

**The lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are my only treasure and the ultimate goal of my life. O deliverer of the fallen souls, please give me the wealth of these lotus feet because I have no one except You.**

**O spiritual master, Lokanātha Gosvāmī, O ocean of mercy, friend of the fallen and the life of all people, please be merciful and give me the shade of your lotus feet. I, Narottama dāsa, take shelter of you alone.**

## Song 10—hari hari! kabe mora haibe sudine

*hari hari! kabe mora haibe sudine*  
*keli-kautūka-raṅge kariba sevane*  
*lalitā-viśākhā sane jateka sakhīra gaṇe*  
*maṇḍalī kariba doṅhā mele*  
*rāi kānu kare dhari nṛtya kare phirī phirī*  
*nirakhi ' goṅāba kutūhalī*  
*alasa-viśrāma-ghare govardhana giri-bare*  
*rāi kānu karibe śayane*  
*narottama dāsa kaya ei jena mora haya*  
*anukṣaṇa caraṇa sevane*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*—O Lord Hari; *kabe*—when; *mora*—my; *haibe—*will be; *sudine—*auspicious day; *keli-kautuka-raṅge—*in the confidential pastimes; *kariba sevane—*I will serve You; *lalitā—*Śrī Lalitā; *viśākhā—*Śrī Viśākhā; *sane—*along with; *jateka—*all the; *sakhīra gane—*groups of the sakhīs; *maṇdalī kariba—*surround; *doṅhā meli—*both of You; *rāi—*Śrī Rādhikā; *kānu—*Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *kare dhari—*holding each other’s hand; *nṛtya kare—*dance; *phirī phirī—*in the circles; *nirakhi—*by seeing; *goṅaba—*spend; *kutūhalī—*eagerly; *alasa—*tired; *viśrāma-ghare—*resting place; *govardhana giri-bare—*Śrī Govardhana Hill; *rāi*—Śrī Rādhikā; *kānu—*Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *karibe śayana—*will lie down; *narottama dāsa—*Narottama dāsa; *kaya—*says; *ei jena*—this; *mora haya—*happens to me; *anukṣaṇa*—always; *caraṇa sevane—*serve Their lotus feet.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari! When will that auspicious day come when I serve You as You enjoy confidential pastimes?**

**When will I, along with Lalitā and Viśākhā and their friends, form a circle around Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa as They hold hands and dance in the middle? As I eagerly gaze upon Them, my voice will become choked up with happiness.**

**After a while, being tired, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa will lie down in a cool resting place on Govardhana Hill. Narottama dāsa prays to always serve Their lotus feet.**

## Song 11—govardhana girivara kevala nirjana sthala

*govardhana girivara kevala nirjana sthala*  
*rāi kānu karibe śayane*  
*lalitā-viśākhā-saṅge sevana kariba raṅge*  
*sukhamaya rātūla caraṇe*  
*kanaka sampūṭa kari karpūra tāmbula bhari*  
*yogāiba badana-kamale*  
*maṇimaya kiṇkiṇī ratna nupūra āni'*  
*parāiba caraṇa-jūgale*  
*kanaka kaṭorā pūri sūgandhi candana bhari*  
*doṅhākāra śrī-aṅge ḍāliba*  
*guru-rūpā sakhībāme tribhaṅga bhaṅgimā ṭhāme*  
*camarera bātāsa kariba*  
*doṅhāra kamala āṅkhi pūlaka haiyā dekhi*  
*duṅhu pada paraśiba kare*  
*caitanya-dāsera dāsa mane mātra abhilāṣa*  
*narottama dāse sadā sphure*

**Пословный перевод:**

*govardhana giri-vara*—Śrī Govardhana Hill; *kevala—*only; *nirjana sthala*—secluded place; *rāi*—Śrī Rādhikā; *kānu—*Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *karibe sayane*—will lie down; *lalitā—*Śrī Lalitā; *viśākhā—*Śrī Viśākhā; *saṅge*—along; *sevana*—serve; *raṅge—*happily; *sukhamaya*—ecstatic; *rātula—*reddish; *caraṇe*—lotus feet; *kanaka sampūṭa kari—*in a golden box; *karpura—*camphor; *tāmbula—*betel nuts; *bhari—*filled with; *jogaiba—*supply; *badana-kamale—*in the lotus mouth; *maṇimaya*—jewelled; *kiṇkiṇi—*ankle bells; *ratana nūpūra—*bedecked with; *āni*—jewels; *parāiba—*bring; *caraṇa-jugale—*decorate the lotus feet; *kanaka—*golden; *katora—*bowl; *bhari—*filled with; *sugandhi—*scented; *candana bhari—*sandalwood paste; *doṅhākāra—*both of Them; *śrī-aṅge—*on Their bodies; *dāliba*—smear; *guru—*the spiritual master; *rūpa*—the form; *sakhī—*a sakhī; *bāme—*on his left; *tribhaṅga bhaṅgima thāme*—threefold bending form; *camarera—*of camara; *bātāsa kariba*—will be fanning; *doṅhāra*—both of Them; *kamala āṅkhi—*lotus eyes; *pūlaka haiyā—*hairs standing on end; *dekhi—*seeing; *duṅhu—*both; *pada—*lotus feet; *paraśiba kare—*touch with hands; *caitanya—*Lord Caitanya; *dāsera dāsa—*servant of His servants; *mane—*in the mind; *mātra—*only; *abhilāṣa—*desire; *narottama dāse—*Narottama dāsa; *sada—*always; *sphure—*aspire.

**Перевод:**

**As Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa relax in a solitary place on Govardhana Hill, I will accompany Lalitā and Viśākhā there to serve Their beautiful lotus feet which offer the benediction of divine bliss.**

**When will I be able to take camphor and betelnuts from a golden box and offer it in Their lotus mouths? O when will I be able to place jeweled ankle bells on Their lotus feet?**

**One auspicious day I will apply sandalwood paste in a golden bowl and apply it on Their limbs. With my guru, who is a sakhi, on my left side, I will take the camara and fan the three-fold bending form of My Lord.**

**I will gaze longingly at Their lotus eyes and become overwhelmed with shivering due to ecstasy, and I will reach out to touch Their lotus feet. Narottama dāsa who is the servant of Lord Caitanya’s servants, has no other desire than this.**

## Song 12—hari hari! āra ki emana daśā haba

*hari hari! āra ki emana daśā haba*  
*kabe bṛṣabhānu-pūre āhīrī gopera ghare*  
*tanayā haiyā janamiba*  
*jāvaṭe āmāra kabe e-pāṇi-grahaṇa habe*  
*vasati kariba kabe tāya*  
*sakhīra parama śreṣṭha je tāhāra haya preṣṭha*  
*sevana kariba tāṅra pāya*  
*teṅha kṛpābān haiñā rātula caraṇe laiñā*  
*āmāre karibe samarpaṇa*  
*saphala haibe daśā pūribe manera āśā*  
*sevi duṅhāra jugala-caraṇa*  
*bṛndābane dui-jana caturdike sakhī-gaṇa*  
*sevana kariba avaśeṣe*  
*sakhī-gaṇa cāribhite nānā jantra laiñā hāte*  
*dekhiba manera abhilāṣe*  
*dūṅhu cāṅda-mukha dekhi' juḍābe tāpita āṅkhi*  
*nayane bahibe aśru-dhāra*  
*vṛndāra nideśa pāba doṅhāra nikaṭe jāba*  
*hena dina haibe āmāra*  
*śrī-rūpa-mañjarī sakhī more anāthinī dekhi*  
*rākhibe rātula duṭī pāya*  
*narottama dāsa bhaṇe priya-narma-sakhī-gaṇe*  
*kabe dāsī karibe āmāra*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*—O Lord Hari; *āra ki—*this ever; *emana daśā haba*—will happen to me; *kabe—*when; *bṛṣabhānu-pure*—in the village of King Vṛṣabhānu; *āhīrī gopera—*of the cowherd man Ahirī; *ghare—*in the house of; *tanayā—*daughter; *haiyā—*being; *janamiba—*will take birth; *jāvate—*in Yavat; *āmāra*—mine; *kabe*—when; *e pāṇi-grahaṇa—*marriage; *habe—*will be; *vasati-kariba—*will reside; *kabe—*when; *tāya—*there; *sakhīra—*of the sakhīs; *parama śreṣṭha—*the best; *je—*whoever; *tāhāra—*her; *haya—*is; *preṣṭha—*intimate; *sevana kariba—*will serve; *tāṅra—*Her; *pāya—*lotus feet; *teṅha—*She; *kṛpābān haiñā—*being merciful; *rātula caraṇe—*reddish lotus feet; *laiñā—*taking; *āmāre—*to me; *karibe samarpaṇa—*will offer; *saphala—*successful; *haibe—*will be; *daśā—*life; *pūribe—*fulfilled; *manera āśā—*have desires; *se duṅhāra—*Their; *jugala-caraṇa—*lotus feet; *bṛndābane—*in Vṛndāvana; *dui-jana—*Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *caturdike—*on four sides; *sakhī-gaṇa*—the sakhīs; *sevana kariba—*will serve; *abaśeṣe—*ultimately; *sakhī—gaṇa—*the sakhīs; *cāribhite*—surrounded; *nānā—*various; *jantra—*instrument; *laiñā hāte—*in their hands; *dekhiba—*will see; *manera abhilāṣe—*heartiest satisfaction; *duṅhu—*both; *cāṅda—*moonlike; *mukha—*face; *dekhi—*seeing; *juḍabe—*will be satisfied; *tāpita āṅkhi—*the burning eyes; *nayane—*in the eyes; *bahibe—*will flow; *aśru-dhāra—*tears; *bṛndāra—*Śrī Vṛndādevī; *nideśa—*instructions; *pāba—*will receive; *doṅhāra—*both of Them; *nikate—*hear; *jāba—*will go; *hena—*such; *dina—*days; *haibe—*will be; *āmāra*—mine; *śrī-rūpa-mañjarī*—Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī; *sakhī—*sakhī; *more*—to me*; anāthinī*—unprotected; *dekhi—*seeing; *rākhibe*—will keep; *rātula duṭī pāya*—in the reddish lotus feet; *narottama dāsa—*Narottama dāsa; *bhaṇe—*laments; *priya-narma—*dearest associates; *sakhī gaṇe—*the sakhīs; *kabe—*when; *dāsī karibe—*make a maidservant; *āmāra—*to me.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, will this ever happen to me that in the village of King Vṛṣabhanu I will take birth as a gopī in the house of Ahirī Gopāla?**

**Living in Yavat I will serve the lotus feet of the intimate friend of Rādhā. Being merciful to me she will engage me in serving Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.**

**By attaining the lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, my life will be successful, and all my desires will be fulfilled.**

**When will I be able to serve the Divine Couple surrounded by Their sakhīs? I will watch to my hearts content the sakhīs with various instruments in their hands.**

**Seeing the moonlike faces of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, my burning eyes will be cooled and tears will flow down. Will this day ever come when Vṛṇda devī will order me to go to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa?**

**Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī sakhī, seeing me unprotected and helpless, will keep me at her reddish lotus feet. Will I become the servant of Śrī Rādhikā’s dearmost sakhī? This is Narottama dāsa’s prayer.**

## Song 13—hari hari! āra ki emana daśā haba

*hari hari! āra ki emana daśā haba*  
*chāḍiyā puruṣa-deha kabe vā prakṛti haba*  
*duṅhu aṅge candana parāba*  
*ṭāniyā bāṅdhiba cūḍā nava guñjā-hāre beḍā*  
*nānā phule gāṅthi diba hāra*  
*pīta-basana aṅge parāiba sakhī-saṅge*  
*badane tāmbūla diba āra*  
*duṅhu rūpa manohārī heriba nayana bhari*  
*nīlāmbare rāi sājāiyā*  
*nava-ratna-jari āni bāṅdhiba vicitra beṇī*  
*tāte phūla mālati gāṅthiyā*  
*sei rūpa-mādhurī dekhiba nayana bhari'*  
*ei kari mane abhilāṣā*  
*jaya rūpa sanātana deha more ei dhana*  
*nivedaye narottama dāsa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari!*—O Lord Hari; *āra ki—*ever; *emana*—such; *daśā—*opportunity; *haba—*will be; *chāḍiyā*—giving up; *puruṣa-deha—*male body; *kabe—*when; *vā prakṛti haba—*will be a female; *duṅhu*—both; *aṅge—*on the limbs; *candana—*sandalwood; *parāba—*will smear; *ṭāniyā—*tightly; *bāṅdhiba—*tie; *cuḍa—*braid; *nava—*new; *guṅjā-hāre*—garland of kuñja; *beḍā—*wrapped; *nānā phule*—with various flowers; *gāṅthi—*make flowers; *diba hāra—*offer; *pīta-basana—*yellow garments; *aṅge—*in the body; *parāiba—*will put; *sakhī-saṅge—*helping the sakhis; *badane*—in the mouth; *tāmbula—*betelnuts; *diba āra—*will also offer; *duṅhu*—Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *rūpa—*form; *manohārī—*enchanting; *heriba*—will see; *nayana bhari*—to the eye’s satisfaction; *nīlāmbare—*sky blue; *rāi—*Śrī Rādhikā; *sājāiyā*—sajaiya; *nava-ratna-jari—*newly jewelled embroidery; *āni—*bring; *bāṅdhiba—*tie; *vicitra—*wonderful; *beṇi—*plates; *tāte—*in that; *phula mālati—*malati flowers; *gāṅthiyā—*decorate; *sei rūpa-madhurī*—that sweet beauty; *dekhiba—*will see; *nayana bhari*—to the eyes satisfaction; *ei kari—*this is the; *mane—*in the mind; *abhilāṣā—*desire; *jaya—*all glories; *rūpa—*Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī; *sanātana—*Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī; *deha more*—please give me; *ei dhana—*this treasure; *nibedaye—*requests; *narottama dāsa—*Narottama dāsa.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, will this ever happen to me that Ileave this male body and upon assuming a female body I will apply sandalwood paste on the limbs of both Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa?**

**I will comb Their hair and decorate Them with new guñja mālā, and make garlands of beautiful flowers for Them, and I will dress Kṛṣṇa in yellow garments, and along with His sakhīs offer betelnuts to Him.**

**I will gaze at the enchanting beauty of Their Lordships and I will dress Śrī Rādhikā with blue garments decorated with nine types of precious gems, and then braid Her hair wonderfully, and offer a garland of mālatī flowers.**

**My desire is to always be gazing at Their beautiful forms. All glories to Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī and Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī. I pray You may give this treasure to Narottama dāsa.**

## Song 14—kusumita vṛndāvane nācata sakhī-gaṇe

*kusumita vṛndāvane nācata sakhī-gaṇe*  
*pika-kula bhramara jhāṅkāre*  
*priya-sahacari saṅge gāiyā jāibe raṅge*  
*manohara nikūñja-kūṭīre*  
*hari hari! manoratha phalibe āmāre*  
*dūṅhuka manthara gati kautuke heraba ati*  
*aṅga bhari pūlaka antare*  
*caudike sakhīra mājhe rādhikāra iṅgite*  
*cirūṇī laiyā kare kari'*  
*kūṭila kūntala saba bikhāriyā āṅcaraba*  
*vanāiba vicitra kabarī*  
*mṛgamada malajaya sabe aṅge lepaba*  
*parāiba manohara hāra*  
*candana kūnkūme tilaka banāiba*  
*heraba mūkha-sudhākara*  
*nīla paṭṭāmbara yatane parāiba*  
*pāye diba ratana-mañjīre*  
*bhṛṅgarera jale rāṅgā- caraṇa dhoyāiba*  
*muchāiba āpana cikure*  
*kusuma-kamala-dale śeṣa bichāiba*  
*śayana karāba doṅhākāre*  
*dhabala camara āni mṛdū mṛdu bījaba*  
*śaramita dūṅhūka śarīre*  
*kanaka sampūṭa kari karpūra tāmbūla bhari*  
*jogāiba doṅhāra vadane*  
*adhara sudhā-rase tāmbūla sūbāse*  
*bhakava adika yatane*  
*śrī-guru karuṇā-sindhu lokanātha dīna-bandhu*  
*mūi dine kare abadhāna*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa vṛndāvana priya-narma-sakhī-gaṇa*  
*narottama māge ei dāna*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kusumita*—full of blooming flowers; *bṛndābane—*in Vṛndāvana; *nācata—*dance; *sakhī-gaṇe*—the sakhīs; *pika-kula—*the birds; *bhramara—*bumblebees; *jhaṅkāre—*humming; *priya sahacarī—*dear-most companion; *saṅge—*along with*; gāiyā—*while singing; *jāibe raṅge—*will walk happily; *manohara—*enchanting; *nikūnja-kūṭīre—*groves of Vṛndāvana; *hari hari—*O Lord Hari; *manoratha—*heartiest desire; *phalibe—*will be successful; *āmāre—*of me; *duṅhuka*—both of them; *manthara gati—*slow movement; *kautuke—*happily; *heraba ati—*will see; *aṅga-bhari—*the whole body; *pūlaka—*shivering; *antare—*internal; *caudike—*on four sides; *sakhīra mājhe—*among the sakhīs; *rādhikāra*—of Śrī Rādhikā; *iṅgite—*by gesture; *cirūṇi*—comb; *laiyā kare kari—*taking in my hand; *kuṭila kūntala—*curly locks of hair; *saba*—all; *bikhariyā aṅcaraba*—comb beautifully; *banāiba*—will make; *vicitra kabarī*—wonderful braids; *mṛgamada—*musk; *malajaya—*sandal pulp; *sabe aṅge—*in the whole body; *lepaba—*will smear; *parāiba*—put on; *manohara—*enchanting; *hārī*—garland; *candana kuṅkuma*—mixed with sandal pulp and kuṅkuma; *tilaka banāiba—*will put tilaka; *heraba—*I will watch; *mūkha-sūdhākara—*the nectarean face; *nīla paṭṭāmbara—*blue garments; *yatane*—carefully; *parāiba—*will put on; *pāye—*in the feet; *diba—*will tie; *ratana-manjīre—*jewelled ankle-bells; *bhṛṅgarera—*a waterpot; *jale—*with the water; *rāṅgā—*with a spout; *caraṇa*—lotus feet; *dhoyāiba*—will wash; *muchaba*—will wipe; *apana cikure*—with my hairs; *kusuma-kamala-dale—*with lotus petals; *śeṣa—*bed; *bichaiba—*will decorate; *śayana karāba doṅhākāre—*make Them lie down; *dhabala—*white; *cāmara—*whisk; *āni—*taking; *mṛdu mṛdu—*gently; *bī jaba—*will fan; *saramita—*shy; *duṅhuka śarīre—*on both Their bodies; *kanaka*—golden box; *sampūṭa kari—*in the hands; *karpūra—*camphor; *tāmbūla—*betel nuts; *bhari—*filling; *jogāiba—*will supply; *doṅhāra*—*badane—*to Their mouths; *adhare*—on the lips; *sudhā-rase—*with the nectar; *tāmbūla subāse—*fragrant betel nuts; *bhakava—*will feed; *adika yatane—*more carefully; *śrī-guru—*the spiritual master; *karuṇā-sindhū—*the ocean of mercy; *lokanātha—*Śrī Lokanātha Gosvāmī; *dina-bandhu—*friend of the poor; *mui dine—*fallen like me; *kare abadhāna—*glance mercifully; *rādhā-kṛṣṇa*—Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *vṛndāvana—*Śrī Vṛndāvana; *priya-narma-sakhī-gaṇa—*dearmost sakhī; *narottama*—Narottama dāsa; *māge—*begs; *ei dāna—*this wealth.

**Перевод:**

**In Vṛndāvana, filled with blooming flowers, dancing peacocks and beautiful sounds of birds and bumblebees, my dear friends and I will go singing to the enchanting cottage in the forest.**

**O Lord Hari, when will this desire be fulfilled that my body will shiver due to great ecstasy as I watch the beautiful movements of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa?**

**When Śrī Rādhikā, surrounded by Her sakhīs, will give a hint, then I will take a comb in my hand and carefully comb Her beautiful curly locks in a wonderful style.**

**I will apply sandalwood paste and musk on Them and offer beautiful garlands. Then Iwill shyly gaze at Their moonlike faces as I apply Their tilaka made from sandalwood paste and kuṁkuma.**

**I will dress Rādhārāṇī with blue silken garments, and place golden anklets on Their lotus feet. Iwill wash Their lotus feet with water from a pot and dry Them with my own hair.**

**Arranging a bed beautifully decorated with lotus flowers I will request Them to lie down and I will fan Them slowly with a white camara.**

**I will take tāmbūla made with camphor and betelnuts from a golden box and place them in Their lotus mouths, fragrant with betelnuts, and thus see Them happy.**

**O Lokanātha Gosvāmī , O my spiritual master, O ocean of mercy and friend of the poor. O master, please glance mercifully on me and bless me to have the service of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, Vṛndāvana Dhāma and the dearmost sakhīs of Kṛṣṇa. These are the innermost desires of Narottama dāsa.**

## Song 15—Punaḥ Svābhīṣṭa-lālasā (Repeated Intense Hankerings)

*hari hari ! kabe mora haibe sudina*  
*govardhana giri-bare parama nibhṛta ghare*  
*rai kānu karāiba śayana*  
*bhṛṅgarera jale raṅgā caraṇa dhoyāiba*  
*muchāba āpana cikure*  
*kanaka-sampūṭa kari karpūra tāmbūla puri*  
*jogāiba dūṅhuka adhare*  
*priya-sakhī-gaṇa-saṅge sevana kariba raṅge*  
*caraṇa seviba nija-kare*  
*duṅhuka kamala-diṭhi kautūke heraba*  
*duṅhu aṅga pūlaka antare*  
*mallikā mālatī jūhī nānā phule mālā gāṅthi*  
*kabe diba doṅhāra galāya*  
*soṇāra kaṭorā kari karpūra candana bhari*  
*kabe diba doṅhākāra gāya*  
*āra kabe emana habe duṅhu-mukha-nirakhiba*  
*līlā-rasa nikūñja-śayane*  
*śrī-kundalatāra saṅge keli-kautuka raṅge*  
*narottama karibe śravaṇe*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*!—O Lord Hari; *kabe*—when; *mora—*for me; *haibe*—will be; *sudina—*auspicious day; *govardhana giri-bare—*on Govardhana Hill; *parama*—almost; *nibhṛta ghare—*secluded place; *rāi—*Śrī Rādhikā; *kānu—*Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *karāiba—*will put; *śayana—*to sleep; *bhṛṅgarera jale*—with the water from a spout; *raṅgā-caraṇa—*reddish lotus feet; *dhoyāiba—*will wash; *muchaba*—will wipe; *āpana cikūre—*with my hairs; *kanaka—*golden; *sampūṭa*—box; *kari—*in the hand; *karpūra*—camphor; *tāmbūla*—betelnuts; *puri—*filled with; *jogāiba—*will supply; *duṅhuka adhare*—in the mouths of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *priya-sakhī-gaṇa—*dearmost sakhīs; *saṅge—*in the association; *sevana kariba*—serve; *raṅge*—happily; *caraṇa seviba—*massage Their feet; *nija-kare—*with my own hands; *duṅhuka—*both of Them; *kamala-diṭhi*—lotus eyes; *kautuke*—jubilantly; *heraba*—I will watch; *dūṅhu-aṅga—*both Their bodies; *pūlaka antare—*shivering internally; *mallikā—*mallikā flowers; *mālatī*—malatī flowers; *jūhī—*juhī flowers; *nānā phule—*with various flowers; *mālā gāṅthi—*make garland; *kabe—*when; *diba—*will offer; *doṅhāra galāya—*on Their necks; *soṇara—*golden bowl; *kaṭorā kari—*in the hands; *karpūra—*camphor; *candana—*sandalwood paste; *bhari—*in it; *kabe*—when; *diba—*will offer; *doṅhākāra gāya*—on Their bodies; *āra kabe—*when will; *emana habe—*this happen; *dūṅhu-mūkha—*both of Their faces; *nirakhiba—*I will see; *līlā-rasa—*the pastimes of; *nikūñja—*groves; *śayane*—lying; *śrī-kūṇḍalatāra saṅge—*with Śrī Kuṇḍalatā; *keli-kautuka—*conjugal pastimes; *raṅge—*happily; *narottama—*Narottama dāsa; *karibe śravaṇe—*will hear.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, when will that auspicious day come that I will make a beautiful couch for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in a secluded flower cottage on Govardhana Hill, who is the best of mountains?**

**When will I wash Their lotus feet with scented water from a waterpot and then dry them with my own hair? When will I take tāmbūla, scented with camphor and betelnuts from a golden box and offer them to the Divine Couple?**

**With my own hands, I will blissfully serve Their lotus feet in the association of dear sakhis. When Igaze upon Their lotus eyes, my whole body will shiver due to ecstatic love.**

**When will I be able to string garlands from mallikā, malatī, yūhī, and various other flowers, and offer these garlands to my Lords? When will I offer tāmbūla, and prepare sandalwood paste and camphor in a golden bowl to apply on Them?**

**When will I be able to see Their lotus faces while They rest in the Nikuñja? Narottama dāsa eagerly hears the pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa from Śrī Kuṇḍalatā.**

## Song 16—Lālasā (Longings)

*śrī-rūpa-mañjarī-pada sei mora sampada*  
*sei mora bhajana-pūjana*  
*sei mora prāṇa-dhana sei mora ābharaṇa*  
*sei mora jīvanera jīvana*  
*sei mora rasa-nidhi sei mora bāñca-siddhi*  
*sei mora vedera dharama*  
*sei vrata, sei tapa sei mora mantra-japa*  
*sei mora dharama-karama*  
*anūkūla habe bidhi se-pade hoibe siddhi*  
*nirakhiba e dui nayane*  
*se rūpa-mādhurī-rāśi prāṇa-kūbalaya śaśi*  
*praphullita habe niśi dine*  
*tuyā adarśana-ahi garale jārala dehi*  
*cira-dina tāpita jīvana*  
*hā hā prabhu! kara dayā deho mora-pada-chāyā*  
*narottama laila śaraṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śrī-rūpa-mañjarī*—Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī; *pada*—lotus feet; *sei mora*—they are my; *sampada—*wealth; *sei mora*—they are my; *bhajana—*devotional service; *pūjana*—worship; *sei mora*—they are my; *prāṇa-dhana—*life and soul; *sei mora*—they are my; *ābharaṇa*—ornaments; *sei mora*—they are my; *jivanera jīvana—*life of my life; *sei mora*—they are my; *rasa-nidhi*—ocean of transcendental mellows; *sei mora*—they are my; *bāñca-siddhi*—fulfillment of desires; *sei mora*—they are my; *vedera dharama—*Vedic religious principles; *sei*—are they; *vrata—*observing vows; *sei—*they are; *tapa—*austerities; *sei mora*—they are my; *mantra-japa—*chanting of the mantras; *sei mora*—they are my; *dharama-karama—*occupational duties; *anukūla*—favourable; *habe—*will be; *bidhi—*regulative devotional service; *se-pade—*by those lotus feet; *hoibe—*will be; *siddhi—*perfection; *nirakhibo*—I will watch; *e dūi nayane*—with these eyes; *se rūpa-mādhurī-raśi—*the exquisite beauty of Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī; *prāṇa—*life; *kubalaya śaśi—*like the moon; *praphullita—*jubilant; *habe—*will be; *niśi dine—*day and night; *tuya—*Your; *adarśana—*separation; *ahi—*the serpent; *garale*—with the poison; *jārala dehi—*throughout my body; *ciro-dina—*permanently; *tāpita—*miserable; *jīvana—*life; *hā hā prabhu!—*O my Lord; *kara dayā—*please be merciful; *deho mora—*give me; *pada-chāyā—*the shelter of Your lotus feet; *narottama—*Narottama dāsa; *laila—*take; *śaraṇa—*shelter.

**Перевод:**

**The lotus feet of Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī are my real wealth. They are the object of my bhajana and pūjā, the treasure of my heart, and they are my ornaments and the very life of my life.**

**They are the reservoirs of all rasa for me and the fulfillment of all my desires. They are the conclusion of the religion of the Vedas for me and are the goal of all my vows, austerities, and the chanting of my mantra. They are the purpose of all my religious activities.**

**By the power of those feet my activities will become favourable to devotion, spiritual perfection will be achieved, and with these two eyes I will be able to actually see. The exquisite beauty of Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī’s divine feet will shine like the brilliant moon upon the lotus of my heart both day and night, thus giving relief to my afflicted soul.**

**The serpent in the form of separation from You has poisoned my whole body, and my life is permanently miserable. O my Lord, please bestow Your mercy upon me. Give me the shade of Your lotus feet. Narottama dāsa takes shelter of You.**

## Song 17—śuniyāchi sādhu-mukhe bale sarva-jana

*śuniyāchi sādhu-mukhe bale sarva-jana*  
*rī-rūpa-kṛpāya mile jugala-caraṇa*  
*hā hā prabhu ! sanātana gaura-paribāra*  
*sabe mili bāñcchā pūrṇa karaha āmāra*  
*śrī-rūpera kṛpā jena āmāra prati haya*  
*se pada āśraya jāra sei mahāśaya*  
*prabhu lokanātha kabe saṅge laiñā jābe*  
*śrī-rūpera pāda-padme more samarpibe*  
*hena ki haibe mora narma-sakhī-gaṇa*  
*anugata narottama karibe śāsane*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śuniyāchi*—I have heard; *sādhu-mukhe—*from the mouth of the devotees; *bale—*says; *sarva-jana*—everyone; *śrī-rūpa-kṛpāya—*by the mercy of Śrī Rūpa; *mile—*one attains; *jugala-caraṇa*—the lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *hā hā prabhu!—*O my Lord; *sanātana—*Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī; *gaura-paribara—*the associates of Gaurāṅga; *sabe mili—*all of you together; *bāñcchā—*desires; *pūrṇa karaha—*fulfill; *āmāra—*mine; *śrī-rūpera—*of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī; *kṛpā—*mercy; *jena—*that; *āmāra prati—*towards me; *haya—*those; *se pada—*those lotus feet; *āśraya—*shelter; *jāra—*whose; *seī—*such a person; *mahāśaya—*a great personality; *prabhū—*O my lord; *lokanātha—*Lokanātha Gosvāmī; *kabe—*when; *saṅge—*with him; *laiñā jābe—*will take me; *śrī-rūpera pāda-padme*—to the lotus feet of Śrī Rūpa; *more—*me; *samarpibe—*will offer; *hena ki—*such auspicious event; *haibe mora—*will happen to me; *narma-sakhī-gaṇa—*the dearmost sakhīs; *anugata—*subordinate; *narottama*—Narottama dāsa; *karibe śāsane—*will chastise.

**Перевод:**

**I have heard from the mouths of pure devotees that by the mercy of Śrī Rūpa Gosvāmī, one can attain the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.**

**O my lord, Sanātana Gosvāmī, and the associates of Gaurāṅga, all of you kindly fulfill my desires.**

**Let the mercy of Śrī Rūpa Gosvāmī be upon me, for whoever has taken shelter of him is my master.**

**When will my lord, Lokanātha Gosvāmī, take me with him, and place me at the lotus feet of Śri Rūpa?**

**Will it ever happen to me that the dearmost sakhīs will chastise their servant, Narottama dāsa?**

## Song 18—ei nava-dāsī bali śrī-rūpa cāhibe

*ei nava-dāsī bali śrī-rūpa cāhibe*  
*hena śubha-kṣaṇa mora kata-dine habe*  
*śīghra ājñā karibena—dāsi hethā āya*  
*sebāra susajjā-kārya karaha tvarāya*  
*ānandita hañā hiyā taṅra ājñā-bale*  
*pavitra mane kārya karibe tat-kāle*  
*sebāra sāmagrī ratna-ṭhālete kariyā*  
*subāsita bāri svarṇa-jhārite pūriyā*  
*doṅhāra sammūkhe la'ye diba śīghra-gati*  
*narottamera daśā kabe haibe e-mati*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ei*—this; *nava—*new; *dāsī—*maidservant; *bali*—considering; *śrī-rūpa*—Śrī Rūpa; *cāhibe—*will gaze; *hena śubha-kṣaṇa*—such auspicious moment; *mora—*mine; *kata-dine habe—*when will it happen; *śīghra—*soon; *ājñā karibena—*instruct; *dāsī*—O maidservant; *hetha āya*—please come here; *sebāra—*of the service; *susajjā-kārya—*the arrangements; *karaha—*prepare; *tvarāya—*quickly; *ānandita hañā*—being jubilant; *hiyā—*heart; *tāṅra—*her; *ājñā-bale*—with the instructions; *pavitra mane—*with a pure mind; *kārya*—activities; *karibe—*will perform; *tat-kāle—*then and there; *sebāra sāmagrī—*the ingredients for service; *ratna-thālete kariyā—*placing on a jewelled plate; *subāsita—*scented; *bāri—*water; *svarṇa—*golden; *jhārite pūriyā*—perforated waterpot; *doṅhāra sammūkhe—*in front of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *la'ye diba—*I will offer; *śīghra-gati—*immediately; *narottamera daśā—*the situation of Narottama dāsa; *kabe haibe e-mati*—when will it be like this.

**Перевод:**

**When will the auspicious moment come, when Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī will accept me as her maidservant?**

**She will order me saying, “Quickly make arrangement for the service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.”**

**With joyful mood and pure mind I will immediately execute that order.**

**Taking the ingredients for Their service on a golden plate along with scented water in a perforated waterpot, I will immediately take them to Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Thus Narottama yearns for such a situation.**

## Song 19—śrī-rūpa paścāte āmi rahiba bhīta hañā

*śrī-rūpa paścāte āmi rahiba bhīta hañā*  
*doṅhe pūnaḥ kahibena āmā pāne cāñā*  
*sadaya hṛdaye doṅhe kahibena hāsi*  
*kothāya pāile rūpa! ei nava dāsī*  
*śrī-rūpa-mañjarī tabe doṅhā vākya śuni*  
*mañjūlālī dila more ei dāsī āni*  
*ati namra-citta āmi ihāre jānila*  
*sebā-kārya diyā tabe hethāya rākhila*  
*hena tattva doṅhākāra sākṣāte kahiyā*  
*narottame sevāya dibe niyūkta kariyā*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śrī-rūpa*—Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī; *paścāte*—behind; *āmi rahiba*—I will remain; *bhīta hañā*—fearfully *doṅhe—*both of Them; *punaḥ—*again; *kahibena—*will order; *āmā pāne*—at me; *cāñā*—by looking; *sadaya hṛdaye—*kind-hearted; *doṅhe—*Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *kahibena—*speak; *hāsi—*smilingly; *kothāya pāile—*where have you got; *rūpa!—*O Rūpa; *ei nava dāsī—*this new maidservant; *śrī-rūpa-mañjarī—*Śrī Rūpa Mañjārī; *tabe—*then; *doṅha bākya śuni—*hearing Their words; *mañjūnālī—*Mañjūlālī; *dila more—*brought me; *ei dāsī āni—*this new maidservant; *ati namra-cita—*very meek and humble; *āmi—*I; *ihāre—*her; *jānila—*understood; *sevā-kārya—*service; *diyā—*by giving; *tabe—*then; *hethāya—*here; *rākhila—*I had kept; *hena tattwa—*such information; *doṅhākāra—*Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *sākṣāte kahiyā—*directly speaking; *narottame—*Narottama dāsa; *sevāya—*in the service; *dibe niyūkta kariyā—*will be engaged.

**Перевод:**

**Seeing me standing in fear behind Śrī Rūpa, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, with kind hearts will again smilingly ask Śrī Rūpa, “Where have you brought this new maidservant from?”**

**Hearing Their statements, Śrī Rūpā will answer: “Mañjūlālī brought her and gave her to me. I could understand her to be very meek and humble, therefore engaging her with service I kept her here”.**

**Śrī Rūpa, after disclosing this information to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, will mercifully engage Narottama dāsa in Their service.**

## Song 20—hā hā ! prabhu lokanātha rākha pāda-dvandve

*hā hā ! prabhu lokanātha rākha pāda-dvandve*  
*kṛpā-dṛṣṭe cāha jadi haiyā ānande*  
*mano-bāñcchā siddhi tabe haṅa purṇa-tṛṣṇa*  
*hethāya caitanya mile sethā rādhā-kṛṣṇa*  
*tumi nā karile dayā ke karibe āra*  
*manera vāsanā purṇa kara ei bāra*  
*e tina saṁsāre mora āra kehā nai*  
*kṛpa kari ‘nija pada-tale deha ṭhāñi*  
*śrī rādhā-kṛṣṇa-līlā-guṇa gāna rātri-dine*  
*śrī narottama-bāṅchā purṇa nahe tuyā bine*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hā hā ! prabhu*—O my lord; *lokanātha—*Lokanātha Gosvāmī; *rākha—*please keep; *pāda-dvandve*—at your lotus feet; *kṛpā-dṛṣṭe—*with merciful reason; *cāha—*please look; *jadi—*if; *haiyā ānande*—being happy; *mano-bāñcchā—*desires; *siddhi—*fulfillment; *tabe—*then; *haṅa—*if I be; *purṇa-tṛṣṇa—*fully eager; *hethaya—*in this world; *caitanya—*Lord Caitanya; *mile—*is attained; *setha—*in the spiritual world; *rādhā-kṛṣṇa*—Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *tumi nā karile—*if do not; *dayā—*merciful; *ke karibe āra—*who else will; *manera vāsanā—*heartiest desires; *purṇa kara—*fulfill; *ei bāra—*this time; *e tina saṁsāre—*in the three worlds; *mora—*mine; *āra kehā nai—*no one else; *kṛpa kari*—mercifully; *nija pada-tale—*under your lotus feet; *deha ṭhāñi—*give me shelter; *śrī rādhā-kṛṣṇa—*Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *līlā—*pastimes; *gūṇa—*qualities; *gāna—*while singing; *rātri-dine—*day and night; *śrī narottama—*Śrīla Narottama dāsa; *bāñchā—*desires; *purṇa*—fulfill; *nahe—*not; *tuyā bine—*without you.

**Перевод:**

**O my lord Lokanātha Gosvāmī, please keep me at your lotus feet. Please mercifully glance upon me.**

**Only by your mercy, my desires of attaining the shelter of Lord Caitanya here, and attaining the shelter of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in the spiritual world, will be fulfilled.**

**If you neglect me, then who else will bestow mercy upon me? Therefore I pray that you kindly fulfill my desires at this time.**

**Please give me shelter under your lotus feet for I have no one else but you in the three worlds.**

**Narottama dāsa wishes to glorify the pastimes and qualities of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa day and night. This is impossible without your mercy.**

## Song 21—lokanātha prabhu ! tumi dayā kara more

*lokanātha prabhu ! tumi dayā kara more*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa jena sadā citte sphure*  
*tomāra sahita thāki sakhīra sahite*  
*ei ta vāsanā mora sadā uṭhe citte*  
*sakhī-gaṇa-jyeṣṭha jeṅho tāṅhāra caraṇe*  
*mora samarpibe kabe sebara kāraṇe*  
*tabe se haibe mora bāñcchita pūraṇa*  
*ānande seviba doṅhāra jūgala caraṇa*  
*śrī-rūpa-mañjarī sakhī! kṛpā-dṛṣṭe cāñā*  
*tāpī narottame siñca sevāmṛta diñā*

**Пословный перевод:**

*lokanātha prabhu !*—O Lokanātha Prabhu; *tumi—*you; *dayā kara more—*please be merciful to me; *rādhā-kṛṣṇa—*Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *caraṇa—*lotus feet; *jena—*may; *sadā—*always; *citte sphure—*manifest in my heart; *tomāra—*your; *sahita—*with; *thāki—*remain; *sakhīra—*of the sakhī; *sahite—*with; *ei ta vāsanā—*this is the desire; *mora—*of me; *sadā—*always; *uṭhe—*appears; *citte—*in my heart; *sakhī-gaṇa—*of the sakhīs; *jyeṣṭha—*senior; *jeṅho*—whoever; *tāṅhāra caraṇe—*unto Her lotus feet; *mora—*to me;*; samarpibe—*will offer; *kabe*—when; *sebara kāraṇe*—for service; *tabe—*then; *se haibe—*that will be; *mora—*mine; *bāñcchita—*desire; *pūraṇa—*fulfillment; *ānande*—in ecstasy; *sebiba*—I will serve; *doṅhāra—*both of Them; *jūgala caraṇa—*lotus feet; *śrī-rūpa-mañjarī sakhī—*Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī sakhī; *kṛpā—*mercifully; *dṛṣṭe cāñā—*glance upon me; *tāpī—*distressed; *narottame—*Narottama dāsa; *siñca—*shower; *sevāmṛta—*nectarean service; *diñā—*by giving.

**Перевод:**

**O my lord Lokanātha, please have mercy on me, so that the lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa will eternally manifest in my mind.**

**This desire to live with you along with the sakhīs, always comes up in my mind.**

**When will you kindly place me at the lotus feet of the senior-most sakhī for rendering loving service?**

**My desires will then be fulfilled and I will engage happily in the service of the lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.**

**O Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī sakhī, please caste your glance of mercy upon me. Please deliver this distressed Narottama dāsa by engaging him in the nectarean service of the Lord.**

## Song 22—hā hā prabhu ! kara dayā karuṇā tomāra

*hā hā prabhu ! kara dayā karuṇā tomāra*  
*michā māyā-jāle tanu dahiche āmāra*  
*kabe hena daśā habe sakhī-saṅga pāba*  
*bṛndābanera phūla gāṅthi doṅhāke parāba*  
*sammukhe basiyā kabe cāmara ḍhulāba*  
*agurū-candana-gandha doṅha aṅge diba*  
*sakhīra ājñāya kabe tāmbūla jogāba*  
*sindūra tilaka kabe doṅhāke parāba*  
*bilāsa-kautuka keli dekhiba nayane*  
*candramūkha nirakhiba basāye siṁhāsane*  
*sadā se mādhurī dekhi manera lālase*  
*kata-dine habe dayā narottama dāse*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hā hā prabhu !*—O my Lord; *kara dayā*—be merciful; *karuṇā*—compassion; *tomāra*—Your; *michā—*false; *māyā-jāi—*network of Māyā; *tanu*—the body; *dahiche*—in burning; *āmāra—*mine; *kabe—*when; *hena—*such; *daśā*—condition; *habe—*will be; *sakhī-saṅga—*the association of the sakhīs; *pāba*—will receive; *bṛndābane*—in Vṛndāvana; *phūla gāṅthi—*making flower garlands; *doṅhāke parāba—*put on Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *sammukhe basiyā—*sitting in front; *kabe—*when; *camara—*the whisk; *ḍhulāba—*will swing; *agurū-candana-gandha*—sandalwood paste with smell of agurū; *doṅha aṅge diba—*smear on Their bodies; *sakhīra ājñāya—*by the instructions of the sakhīs; *kabe—*when; *tāmbūla—*betelnuts; *jogāba—*will supply; *sindūra—*sindūra; *tilaka—*tilaka; *kabe—*when; *doṅhake parāba—*put on Them; *bilāsa—*pastimes; *kautuka keli*—conjugal affairs; *dekhiba—*I will see; *nayane*—in my eyes; *candramūkha*—a moonlike face; *nirakhiba*—I will see; *basāye*—make Them sit; *siṁhāsane—*on the throne; *sadā—*always; *se mādhurī—*that sweetness; *dekhi—*seeing; *manera lālase—*greediness of the mind; *kata-dine habe—*when will it be; *dayā—*compassionate; *narottama dāse*—towards Narottama dāsa.

**Перевод:**

**O my Lord, please be compassionate on me. My body is always burning in the false network of Māyā!**

**When will I be fortunate enough to have the association of the sakhīs in Vṛndāvana, making flower garlands and decorating both of You?**

**When will I fan both of You with a camara and put fragrant sandalwood paste on Your bodies?**

**When will I offer You betelnuts by the order of the sakhīs and put sindhūra and tilaka on You?**

**When will I see Your beautiful pastimes with my own eyes, as well as Your moonlike faces after having positioned You on the throne?**

**When will the Lord, being kind to me, allow me to always see the sweet pastimes for which I, Narottama dāsa, am extremely greedy?**

## Song 23—hari hari ! kabe hena daśā habe mora

*hari hari ! kabe hena daśā habe mora*  
*seviba doṅhāra pada ānande vibhora*  
*bhramara haiyā sadā rahiba caraṇe*  
*śrī-caraṇāmṛta sadā kariba āsvādane*  
*ei āśā kari āmi jata sakhī-gaṇa*  
*tomādera kṛpāya haya bāñcchita pūraṇa*  
*bahu-dina bāñcchā kari pūrṇa jāte haya*  
*sabe meli dayā kara haiyā sadaya*  
*sevā āśe narottama kāṇḍe dibā-niśi*  
*kṛpā kari’ kara more anūgata dāsī*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari !*—O Lord Hari; *kabe—*when; *hena—*such; *daśā—*the status; *habe mora—*I will be; *seviba—*I will serve; *doṅhāra pada—*the lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *ānande—*in bliss; *vibhora—*being absorbed; *bhramara*—bumblebee; *haiyā*—being; *sadā*—always; *rahiba*—remain; *caraṇe*—on the lotus feet; *śrī-caraṇāmṛta—*the nectar of the lotus feet; *sadā—*constantly; *kariba āsvādane—*I will relish; *ei āśā kari—*this desire; *āmi—*I am; *jata sakhī-gaṇa—*all the sakhīs; *tomādera kṛpāya—*Your mercy; *haya—*is; *bāñcchita*—desire; *pūraṇa—*fulfilled; *bahu-dina—*for a long time; *bāñcchā kari—*I desire; *pūrṇa jāte haya—*may be fulfilled; *sabe meli—*all together; *dayā kara—*be merciful; *haiyā sadaya—*be compassionate; *sevā āśe—*with the desire of service; *narottama—*Narottama dāsa; *kaṇḍe—*cries; *dibā-niśi—*day and night; *kṛpā kari—*be merciful; *kara more—*make me; *anūgata dāsī—*a subordinate maidservant.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, when will I have the fortune to serve the lotus feet of the Divine Couple, being completely absorbed in bliss?**

**Being a bumblebee, I will always remain at Their lotus feet and enjoy Their nectar.**

**This is my only hope: O my dear sakhīs, only by the mercy of all of you can this be fulfilled.**

**I desired this for a very long time, therefore being kind-hearted, please have mercy on me and fulfill it.**

**Narottama dāsa weeps day and night for want of devotional service, so please make me Your obedient and faithful maidservant.**

## Song 24—jaya jaya śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya nityānanda

*jaya jaya śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya nityānanda*  
*jayādvaitacandra jaya gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*  
*kṛpā kari sabe meli karaha karuṇā*  
*adhama patita-jane nā kariha ghṛṇ*  
*e tina-saṁsāra-mājhe tuyā pada sāra*  
*bhāviyā dekhinū mane-gati nāhi āra*  
*se pada pābāra āśe kheda uṭhe mane*  
*byākūla hṛdaya sadā kariye krandane*  
*ki-rūpe pāiba kichu nā pāi sandhāna*  
*prabhu-lokanātha pada nāhika smaraṇa*  
*tūmi ta dayālu prabhu! cāha eka bāra*  
*narottama hṛdayera ghucā-o andhakāra*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya jaya śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya*—all glories to Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya; *nityānanda*—Lord Nityānanda; *jayādvaitacandra—*all glories to Advaita Ācārya; *jaya gaura-bhakta-vṛnda—*all glories to the devotees of Gaurāṅga; *kṛpā kari*—be merciful; *sabe meli—*all of you; *karaha karuṇā—*bestow mercy; *adhama—*fallen; *patita—*wretched; *jane*—person; *nā kariha—*do not; *ghṛṇā*—hate; *e tina-saṁsāra-mājhe—*within the three worlds; *tūyā—*Your; *pada—*lotus feet; *sāra—*essence; *bhāviyā dekhinu—*thought carefully; *mane—*in the mind; *gati*—alternative; *nāhi āra—*no other; *se pada*—those lotus feet; *pābāra āśe—*in order to achieve; *kheda—*lament; *uṭhe—*appears; *mane—*in the mind; *byākula—*overwhelmed; *hṛdaya—*heart; *sadā—*always; *kariye krandane—*cry; *ki-rūpe—*how; *pāiba—*I will receive; *kichu nā—*nothing; *pāi—*get; *sandhāna*—information; *prabhū-lokanātha pada*—the lotus feet of Lokanātha Gosvāmī; *nāhika smaraṇa—*I cannot remember; *tūmi ta—*You are; *dayālu—*merciful; *prabhu!—*O Lord; *cāha—*please look upon; *eka-bāra*—once; *narottama—*Narottama dāsa; *hṛdayera—*of the heart; *ghucā-o—*destroy; *andhakāra—*darkness.

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda! All glories to Śrī Advaita, and all glories to all the devotees of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu!**

**All of You, kindly be compassionate on me and do not reject this most fallen soul.**

**I have carefully considered and concluded that Your lotus feet are the only essence in this material world. There is no other way.**

**To obtain the shelter of those lotus feet, I always lament. My heart becomes overwhelmed, thus I cry.**

**I have neither information of how to obtain them nor do I remember the lotus feet of my spiritual master, Lokanātha Gosvāmī.**

**You are the most merciful Lord. Therefore, please keep Your merciful vision on me and dissipate the darkness of the heart of Narottama dāsa.**

## Song 25—Sādhaka-dehocita Lālasā (The Devotee's Feelings of Separation)

*hari hari! kabe mora haibe sudina*  
*bhajiba śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇa hañā premādhīna*  
*sujantre miśāyā gāba sumadhura tāna*  
*ānande kariba doṅhāra rūpa-guṇa-gāna*  
*‘rādhikā govinda’ bali kāndiba uccaiḥ-svare*  
*bhijibe sakala aṅga nayanera nīre*  
*ei-bāra karuṇā kara rūpa sanātana*  
*raghunātha dāsa mora śrī-jīva-jīvana*  
*ei bāra karuṇā kara lalitā-viśākhā*  
*sakhya-bhāve śrīdama-subala-adi sakhā*  
*sabe mili kara dayā purūka mora āśa*  
*prārthanā karaye sadā narottama dāsa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*—O Lord Hari; *kabe*—when; *mora—*mine; *haibe—*will be; *sudina—*auspicious day; *bhajiba—*I will see; *śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇa—*Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *haiyā premādhīna*—being controlled by love; *sujantre—*instrument; *miśāyā—*with mixing; *gāba—*I will sing; *su-madhura tāna—*sweet tune; *ānande—*with happiness; *kariba—*I will do; *duṅhāra—*both of Them; *rūpa—*beauty; *guṇa—*qualities; *gāna—*sing; *rādhikā—*Śrī Rādhikā; *govinda—*Śrī Govinda;*’ bali—*while chanting; *kāndiba*—I will cry; *uccaiḥ-svare—*loudly; *bhijiba—*will be wet; *sakala aṅga—*whole body; *nayanera nīre—*with the tears; *ei-bāra—*once; *karuṇā kara—*be compassionate; *rūpa—*O Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī; *sanātana—*O Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī; *raghunātha dāsa—*O Śrīla Raghunātha dāsa Gosvāmī; *mora—*mine; *śrī-jīva—*O Śrīla Jīva Gosvāmī; *jīvana*—life and soul; *ei bāra*—this time; *karuṇā kara—*be merciful; *lalitā—*O Lalitā*; viśākhā—*O Viśākhā; *sakhya-bhave—*of friendship; *śrīdama—*Śrīdama; *subala—*Subala; *adi—*etc.; *sakhā—*friends; *sabe mili—*all of you; *kara dayā—*be merciful; *purūka—*let it fulfill; *mora āśa—*my desires; *prārthanā karaye—*prayer; *sadā—*always; *narottama dāsa—*Narottama dāsa;

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, when will that auspicious day arrive, when I will worship Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, being absorbed in love of God?**

**I will sing a sweet tune, mixing it with the sound of a beautiful musical instrument and with ecstasy glorify the form and qualities of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.**

**I will cry loudly saying “Rādhikā, Govinda” and my whole body will be wet with tears streaming from my eyes.**

**O my lords of my life, Śrī Rūpa, Sanātana, Raghunātha dāsa and Śrī Jīva; please be merciful on me this time!**

**O Lalitā, O Viśākhā, please be merciful to me this time! O Śrīdamā, Subala and all the sakhās of Kṛṣṇa, please be merciful on me!**

**All of you kindly bless me so that my desires be fulfilled, thus Narottama dāsa always prays.**

## Song 26—Sādhaka-dehocita Śrī Vṛndāvana Vāsa Lālasā (The Devotee's Aspiration to Reside in Śrī Vṛndāvana)

*hari hari ! āra ki emana daśā haba*  
*e bhava saṁsāra tyaji parama ānande maji*  
*āra kabe brajabhūme jāba*  
*sukhamaya vṛndāvana kabe habe daraśana*  
*se dhūli lāgibe kabe gāya*  
*preme gadagada haiñā rādhā-kṛṣṇa-nāma laiñā*  
*kāṅdiyā beḍāiba ubharāya*  
*nibhṛta nikuñja jāñā aṣṭāṅge praṇāma haiñā*  
*ḍākiba hā rādhā-nātha ! bali*  
*kabe jamunāra tīre paraśa kariba nīre*  
*kabe piba kara-puṭe tuli*  
*āra kabe emana ha’ba śrī-rāsa-maṇḍale jāba*  
*kabe gaḍagaḍi diba tāya*  
*vaṁśīvaṭa-chāyā pāñā parama ānanda hañā*  
*paḍiyā rahiba tāra chāya*  
*kabe govardhana giri dekhiba nayana bhari*  
*kabe habe rādhā-kuṇḍe vāsa*  
*bhramite bhramite kabe e deha patana habe*  
*kahe dīna narottama dāsa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari!*—O Lord Hari; *āra ki emana—*even such; *daśā—*such; *haba—*will have; *e bhava saṁsāra—*this material world; *tyaji—*by giving up; *parama ānande—*in supreme bliss; *maji—*absorb; *āra kabe—*when will; *brajabhūme—*in the land of Braja; *jāba—*go; *sukhamaya vṛndāvana—*the most blissful Vṛndāvana; *kabe—*when; *habe daraśana—*will I see; *se dhūli—*the dust; *lāgibe kabe gāya—*feel my body; *preme*—with love; *gaḍagaḍa haiñā—*being choked; *rādhā-kṛṣṇa-nāma*—holy names of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *laiñā—*chanting; *kāṅdiyā—*cry; *beḍāiba—*wander; *ubharāya*—loudly; *nibhṛte—*secretly; *nikuñja jānā—*going; *aṣṭāṅge—*falling like a rod; *praṇāma haiyā—*offer obeisances; *ḍākiba—*I will call out; *hā rādhā-nātha ! bali—*O Lord of Rādhā; *kabe—*when; *jamunāra tīre*—on the bank of Yamunā; *paraśa kariba nīre—*I will touch the water; *kabe piba—*when will I drink; *kara-puṭe tuli—*palmful of water; *āra kabe emana haba—*when will I become; *śrī-rasa-maṇḍale—*in the Rasa-līlā arena; *jāba—*I will go; *kabe—*when; *gaḍagaḍi diba—*will roll; *tāya—*on that ground; *vaṁśīvaṭa—*in Vaṁśīvaṭa; *chāyā pāñā—*receiving the cooling shadow; *parama ānanda hañā—*will be in ecstasy; *paḍiyā—*falling; *rahiba—*I will remain; *tāra chāya—*in its shadow; *kabe—*when; *govardhana giri—*Govardhana Hill; *dekhiba—*I will see; *nayana bhari—*to my eyes satisfaction; *kabe habe—*when will I; *rādhā-kuṇḍe—*at Rādhā-kuṇḍa; *vāsa—*reside; *bhramite bhramite—*while travelling; *kabe e deha*—when this body; *patana habe*—will be giving up; *kahe*—says; *dīna—*poor; *narottama dāsa—*Narottama dāsa.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, when will that day come when I leave this material world and go to the land of Vraja, absorbed in transcendental bliss?**

**When will I smear my body with the dust of blissful Vṛndāvana? I will wander about Vṛndāvana chanting the names of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in a voice choked with ecstatic love.**

**Going to a solitary place at Nikuñja forest, I will offer my full obeisances and call out, “O Rādhānātha”! When will I go to the bank of the Yamunā and drink a palmful of water?**

**When will that day come that I enter Śrī Rāsāmaṇḍala and roll on the ground in ecstasy? When will I stay under the shade of Vaṁśīvaṭa in complete transcendental bliss?**

**When will I see Govardhana Hill, my eyes brimming with tears, and when will I be able to live at Rādhā-kuṇḍa? When, after wandering around Vṛndāvana in this way, will my body collapsed? Poor unfortunate Narottama dāsa thus laments.**

## Song 27—hari hari ! āra kabe pālaṭibe daśā

*hari hari ! āra kabe pālaṭibe daśā*  
*e saba kariyā bāme jāba vṛndāvana-dhāme*  
*ei mane kariyāchi āśā*  
*dhana jana pūtra dvāre e saba kariyā dūre*  
*ekānta haiyā kabe jāba*  
*saba duḥkha parihari vṛndāvane bāsa kari*  
*madhukarī māgiyā khāiba*  
*jamunāra jala jena amṛta samāna hena*  
*kabe piba udara pūriyā*  
*kabe rādhā-kuṇḍa-jale snāna kari kutūhale*  
*śyama-kuṇḍe rahiba paḍiyā*  
*hhramiba dvādaśa bane rasa-keli je je sthāne*  
*premābeśe gaḍagaḍi diyā*  
*sudhāiba jane jane vraja-bāsī-gaṇa-sthāne*  
*nivediba caraṇa dhariyā*  
*bhojanera sthāna kabe nayana-gocara habe*  
*āra jata āche upabana*  
*tāra madhye vṛndāvana narottama dāsera mana*  
*āśā kare jugala caraṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari !*—O Lord Hari; *āra kabe*—when will; *pālaṭibe—*my; *daśā—*condition; *e saba kariyā bāme—*leave aside everything; *jāba—*go; *vṛndāvana-dhāme—*to Vṛndāvana; *ei mane—*in my mind; *kariyāchi āśā—*I have decided; *dhana—*wealth; *jana—*followers; *putra—*children; *dvāre—*wife; *e saba—*everything; *kariyā dūre*—will leave aside; *ekānta haiyā—*being alone; *kabe jāba—*when will I go; *saba duḥkha—*all miseries; *parihari—*giving up; *vṛndāvane—*in Vṛndāvana; *bāsa kori—*reside; *madhukarī—*beg like a bumblebee; *māgiyā khāiba—*I will eat; *jamunāra*—of Yamunā; *jala jena—*the water; *amṛta—*nectar; *samāna hena*—equal to; *kabe-piba*—when will I drink; *udara puriyā—*filling my belly; *kabe—*when; *rādhā-kuṇḍa-jale*—in the waters of Rādhā-kuṇḍa; *snāna kari—*take bath; *kutūhale—*happily; *śyama-kuṇḍe—*in the waters of Śyāma-kuṇḍa; *rahiba paḍiyā—*I will remain; *bhramiba—*will travel; *dvādaśa bane—*in the twelve forests; *rasa-keli—*the nectarean conjugal pastimes; *je je sthāne—*wherever took place; *premābeśe—*being absorbed in love; *gaḍagaḍi diyā—*roll on the ground; *sudhāiba—*will inquire; *jane jane—*person to person; *vraja-bāsī-gaṇa-sthāne—*from the residents of Vṛndāvana; *nivediba—*I will request; *caraṇa dhariyā—*holding their feet; *bhojanera sthāna—*the place where Śrī Kṛṣṇa ate; *kabe*—when; *nayana-gocara habe—*I will see; *āra jata āche—*all other; *upabana—*sub-forests; *tāra madhye—*among them; *vṛndāvana—*Vṛndāvana; *narottama dāsera mana—*the mind of Narottama dāsa; *āśā kare—*desires; *jugala caraṇa—*the lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, when will my condition change so that I can abandon everything and go to Vṛndāvana?**

**When will I give up wealth, followers, sons, wife and go to Vṛndāvana? When will I forget all my distresses and simply reside in Vṛndāvana? When will I maintain my livelihood by begging like a bee?**

**When will I consider the water of Yamunā as nectarean and drink to my full satisfaction? When will I take bath in the waters of Rādhā-kuṇḍā and lie down on the banks of Śyāma-kuṇḍa?**

**When will I travel to the twelve forests where various pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa took place and roll on the ground? When will I fall at the feet of the Vrajāvāsīs and inquire about the locations of the pastimes?**

**When will I see the place where Kṛṣṇa ate with His cowherd boy-friends? When will I visit the forests and sub-forests of Vṛndāvana? Hankering in this way, Narottama dāsa desires the lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.**

## Song 28—karaṅga kaupīna lañā cheṅḍa kāṅthā gāya diyā

*karaṅga kaupīna lañā cheṅḍa kāṅthā gāya diyā*  
*teyāgiba sakala biṣayā*  
*kṛṣṇe anurāga habe brajera nikuñje kabe*  
*jāiyā kariba nijālaya*  
*hari hari! kabe mora haibe su-dina !*  
*phalamūla bṛndābane khāñā dibā-abasāne*  
*bhramiva haiyā udāsīna*  
*śītala jamunā jale snāna kari kūtūhale*  
*premābeśe ānandita hañā*  
*bāhura upara bāhu tuli vṛndāvane kuli kuli*  
*kṛṣṇa bali beḍāba kāṅdiyā*  
*dekhiba saṅketa-sthāna juḍābe tāpita prāṇa*  
*premābeśe gaḍagaḍi diba*  
*kāṅhā rādhā! prāṇeśvari! kāṅhā giribara-dhāri!*  
*kāṅhā nātha ! baliyā ḍākiba*  
*mādhavī-kuñjera pari sukhe basī śuka-śārī*  
*gāibeka rādhā-kṛṣṇa-rasa*  
*taru-mūle basi tāhā śuni juḍāiba hiyā*  
*kabe sukhe goñāba dibasa*  
*śrī-govinda gopīnātha śrīmatī-rādhikā-sātha*  
*dekhiba ratana-siṁhāsane*  
*dīna narottama dāsa karaye durlabha āśa*  
*e-mati haibe kata dine*

**Пословный перевод:**

*karaṅga*—waterpot; *kaupīna—*loincloth; *lañā—*taking; *cheṅḍa—*torn; *kāṅthā*—quilt; *gāya diyā—*covered the body; *teyāgiba—*I will give up; *sakala viṣayā—*all sense objects; *kṛṣṇa—*unto Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *anurāga habe—*will be attached; *brajera*—of Braja; *nikuñje*—in the groves of; *kabe—*when; *jāñā*—going; *kariba—*built; *nijālaya—*my cottage; *hari hari!*—O Lord Hari; *kabe*—when will; *mora haibe su-dina!—*that auspicious day come; *phalamūla—*fruits and nuts; *vṛndāvane—*in Vṛndāvana; *khāiyā—*eating; *dibā-abasāne—*at the end of the day; *bhramiba—*I will travel; *haiyā udāsīna—*being detached; *śītala—*cooling; *jamunā jale—*waters of Yamunā; *snāna kari—*bath; *kutūhale—*happily; *premābeśe*—with ecstasy; *ānandita hannā—*and jubilation; *bāhura upara bāhu tuli—*raising the hands; *bṛndābane kuli kuli—*in the streets of Vṛndāvana; *kṛṣṇa bali*—chanting the names of Kṛṣṇa; *beḍāba—*will wander; *kāṅdiyā—*while crying; *dekhiba—*I will see; *saṅketa-sthāna—*Saṅketa*,* the meeting place of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *juḍābe*—will be pacified; *tāpita prāṇa—*miserable life; *premābeśe—*in ecstasy; *gaḍagaḍi diba—*roll on the ground; *kāṅhā—*where is; *rādhā—*Śrī Rādhā; *prāṇeśvari—*my life and soul; *kāṅhā—*where is; *giribara-dhāri—*the lifter of Govardhana Hill; *kāṅhā nātha*—where is the Lord; *baliyā ḍākiba—*I will call out; *mādhavī-kunjera pari—*in the Mādhavī grove; *sukhe—*happily; *basi—*reside; *śuka-śārī—*male and female parrots; *gāibeka—*will sing; *rādhā-kṛṣṇa-rasa*—the pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *taru-mūle—*under the tree; *basī tāhā—*sitting there; *śuni—*hearing; *juḍāiba hiyā*—pacify my body; *kabe—*when; *sukhe—*in happiness; *goñāba dibasa—*spend the day; *śrī-govinda—*Śrī Govinda; *gopīnātha—*Śrī Gopīnātha; *śrīmatī-rādhikā—*Śrī Rādhikā; *sātha—*along with; *dekhiba—*I will see; *ratana-siṁhāsane—*on a golden throne; *dīna—*poor; *narottama dāsa—*Narottama dāsa; *karaye durlabha āśa—*rare desires; *e-mati—*like this; *haibe kata dine—*when will it happen.

**Перевод:**

**When will I give up all material sense gratification and accept only a waterpot, loincloth and torn garments? When will I develop attachment for Kṛṣṇa and go to live in the nikuñja groves of Braja? O Lord Hari, when will that auspicious day come?**

**When will that day be mine that I wander in Vṛndāvana like a mendicant eating only fruits and roots at the end of the day?**

**When will I take bath in the Yamunā in loving ecstasy, and wander about Vṛndāvana with my hands raised, crying out the names of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa?**

**As soon as I see Saṅketa, the secret meeting place of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, I will roll on the ground with love and my burning life will be satiated. I will call out, “Where is Śrī Rādhā, my life and soul, and where is Giribara-dhārī, my beloved Lord?”**

**As the male and female parrots sing of the loving pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa while sitting on a Mādhavī kuñja, I will sit under the tree the whole day listening to them, and my burning heart will find relief.**

**When will I see my Lords Govinda Gopīnātha and Śrīmatī Rādhikā as They happily sit together on a jewelled throne? Poor Narottama dāsa prays for such rare vision.**

## Song 29—hari hari ! kabe haba vṛndāvana-vāsī

*hari hari ! kabe haba vṛndāvana-vāsī*  
*nirakhiba nayane jugala-rūpa-rāśi*  
*tyajiyā śayana-sukha vicitra pālañka*  
*kabe brajera dhūlāya dūsara habe anga*  
*ṣaḍa-rasa-bhojana dūre parihari*  
*kabe braje māgiyā khāiba mādhukarī*  
*parikramā kariyā beḍāba vane vane*  
*viśrāma kariba jāi jamunā-puline*  
*tāpa dūra kariba śītala vaṁśīvaṭe*  
*kabe kuñje baiṭhaba hāma vaiṣṇava-nikaṭe*  
*narottama dāsa kahe karī parihāra*  
*kabe vā emana daśā haibe āmāra*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*!—O Lord Hari; *kabe haba—*when will I be; *vṛndāvana-vāsī—*resident of Vṛndāvana; *nirakhiba—*I will see; *nayane—*in the eyes; *jugala-rūpa-rāśi—*the beauty of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *tyajiyā—*giving up; *śayana-sukha—*the pleasure of sleeping; *vicitra—*wonderful; *pālañka—*couch; *kabe*—when; *brajera dhūlāya—*in the dust of Vṛndāvana; *dūsara—*grey; *habe—*will be; *aṅga—*limbs; *ṣada-rasa-bhojana—*six kinds of palatable foodstuffs; *dūre parihari*—abandon; *kabe—*when; *braje—*in Braja; *māgiyā—*beg; *khāiba*—will eat; *mādhukarī—*like a honey bee; *parikramā kariyā beḍāba—*I will circumambulate; *bane bane—*different forests; *viśrāma kariba*—I will rest; *jāi—*go; *jamunā-puline—*on the banks of Yamunā; *tāpa dūra kariba—*cool myself; *śītala vaṁśīvaṭe—*the cooling shades of Vaṁṣībaṭa; *kabe—*when; *kuñje—*in the groves; *baiṭhaba—*sit down; *hāma—*myself; *vaiṣṇava-nikate—*in the association of the vaiṣṇavas; *narottama dāsa* –Narottama dāsa; *kahe—*says; *karī parihāra—*giving up everything; *kabe vā—*when; *emana daśā haibe—*when will there be such a day; *āmāra—*for me

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari, when will I reside in Vṛndāvana and behold the beauty of the Divine Couple, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa?**

**When will I give up the happiness of sleeping on a luxurious bed and when will I smear my body with the dust of Braja?**

**When will I give up enjoying the six palatable foodstuffs and when will I beg “madhukarī” like a bee in Braja to maintain my body?**

**When will I wander through forest to forest, doing parikramā? And when I am tired, will I take rest on the banks of Yamunā?**

**I will cool myself down beneath the shade of Vaṁśīvaṭa, and sit in the kuñja with other Vaiṣṇavas.**

**Narottama dāsa says: “When will that day come that I actually give up everything?”**

## Song 30—Sa-vilāpa - Śrī Vṛndāvana-vāsa-lālasā (Lament for Resident in Śrī Vṛndāvana)

*āra ki emana daśā haba*  
*saba chāḍi vṛndāvane jāba*  
*āra kabe śrī-rāsa-maṇḍale*  
*gaḍagaḍi diba kūtūhale*  
*āra kabe govardhana giri*  
*dekhiba nayana-juga bhari*  
*śyāma-kuṇḍe rādhā-kuṇḍe snāna*  
*kari kabe juḍāba parāṇa*  
*āra kabe jamunāra jale*  
*majjane haiba niramale*  
*sādhu-saṅge vṛndāvane vāsa*  
*narottama dāsa kare āśa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*āra ki emana*—will it ever; *daśā haba*—happens to me; *saba chāḍi—*giving up everything; *vṛndāvane jāba*—I will go to Vṛndāvana; *āra kabe—*when will; *śrī-rāsa-maṇḍale—*in the rāsa-līlā arena; *gaḍagaḍi diba—*roll on the ground; *kūtūhale—*happily; *āra kabe*—when will; *govardhana giri*—on the hills of Govardhana; *dekhiba—*I will see; *nayana-juga bhari—*to my eyes’ satisfaction; *śyāma-kuṇḍe—*in Śyāma-kuṇḍa; *rādhā-kuṇḍe—*in Rādhā-kuṇḍa; *snāna—*bathe; *kari kabe—*when; *juḍāba—*pacify; *parāṇa—*my life; *āra kabe—*when; *jamunāra jale—*in the waters of Yamunā; *majjane*—by bathing; *haiba—*I will be; *niramale—*purified; *sādhu-saṅge—*in the association of the devotees; *vṛndāvane vāsa—*reside in Vṛndāvana; *narottama dāsa*—Narottama dāsa; *kare āśa*—desires.

**Перевод:**

**O when will it ever happen that I will abandon everything and go to Vṛndāvana?**

**When will I eagerly roll on the ground where the rāsalīlā pastimes took place? When will I see Govardhana Hill to my full satisfaction?**

**When will I bathe at Śyāma-kuṇḍa and Rādhā-kuṇḍa and relieve my soul? When will I bathe in Yamunā and purify myself?**

**Narottama dāsa longs and aspires to live in Vṛndāvana in the association of devotees.**

## Song 31—Mathura-virahocita Darśana-lālasā (Devotee’s Yearning to see Mathurā)

*kabe kṛṣṇa-dhana pāba hiyāra mājhāre thoba*  
*juḍāiba tāpita-parāṇa*  
*sājāiyā diba hiyā basāiba prāṇa-priyā*  
*nirakhiba se candra-bayāna*  
*he sajani ! kabe mora haibe su-dina*  
*se prāṇa-nāthera saṅge kabe vā phiriba raṅge*  
*sukhamaya jamunā-pulina*  
*lalitā viśākhā lañā tāṅhāre bheṭiba giyā*  
*sājāiyā nānā upahāra*  
*sadaya haiyā bidhi milāibe guṇa-nidhihena bhāgya haibe āmāra*  
*dāruṇa bidhira nāṭa bhāṅgila premera hāṭa*  
*tila-mātra nā rākhila tā’ra*  
*kahe narottama dāsa ki mora jivane āśa*  
*chāḍi gela brajendra-kumāra*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kabe*—when; *kṛṣṇa-dhana—*treasure in the form of Kṛṣṇa; *pāba—*I will receive; *hiyāra mājhāre*—within the heart; *thoba—*I will keep; *juḍāiba*—pacify; *tāpita-parāṇa—*miserable life; *sājāiyā diba—*I will decorate; *hiyā—*the body; *basāiba—*make Him seat; *prāṇa-priyā—*the Lord of my life; *nirakhiba—*I will gaze; *se candra-bayāna—*that moonlike face; *he sajani!—*O dear friend; *kabe—*when; *mora—*mine; *haibe su-dina—*will be auspicious; *se prāṇa-nāthera saṅge*—with the dearmost Lord; *kabe vā—*when; *phiriba—*wander; *raṅge—*happily; *sukhamaya—*blissful; *jamunā-pulina—*banks of Yamunā; *lalitā viśākhā lañā—*with Lalitā and Viśākhā; *tāṅhāre—*to Them; *bheṭiba giyā—*I will go and meet; *sājāiyā nānā*—carrying various; *upahāra—*gifts; *sadaya haiyā—*being merciful; *bidhi—*Providence; *milāibe—*arrange; *guṇa-nidhi*—ocean of transcendental qualities; *hena bhāgya—*such fortune; *haibe āmāra—*will be mine; *dāruṇa—*stringent; *bidhira nāṭa—*laws of providence; *bhāṅgila—*scattered; *premera hāṭa—*the market place of love of God; *tila mātra—*even a bit; *nā rākhila—*did not keep; *tā’ra—*of that; *kahe—*says; *narottama dāsa—*Narottama dāsa; *ki—*what; *mora—*mine; *jibane—*in life; *āśa*—hope; *chaḍi gela—*left me; *brajendra-kumāra—*the son of the King of Braja.

**Перевод:**
