*mṛgamada-candana-kuṅkume bhela paṅka*  
*dig vidig nāhi premera pāthāra*  
*dubila narottama nā jāne saṅtara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*āji—*today; *rase*—in the mellows; *bādara—*rain; *niśi—*night; *preme—*in the love; *bhāsala—*float; *saba—*all; *vṛndavāna-vāsī—*residents of Vṛndāvana; *śyāma—*dark; *ghana—*thick; *barikhaye—*showers; *prema—*love; *sudhā—*nectar; *dhāra—*flow; *kore—*does; *raṅgiṇī—*sporting; *rādhā—*Śrīmatī Rādhāraṇī; *bijurī-saṅcāra—*cause lightening; *preme*—with love; *pichala—*slippery; *patha—*the path; *gamana*—walking; *bhela—*was; *baṅka*—crooked; *mṛgamada—*musk; *candana—*sandalwood paste; *kuṅkume—*with red paste; *bhela—*was *paṅka—*mud; *dig vidig—*every direction; *nāhi—*not *premera—*of love; *pāthāra—*ocean; *dubila—*drowned; *narottama*—Narottama dāsa; *nā—*not; *jāne—*knows; *saṅtara—*swimming.

**Перевод:**

**All the residents of Vṛndāvana floated tonight in the heavy rain of divine loving mellows.**

**Śrī Kṛṣṇa is showering the condensed nectarean flow of love, and the sporting of Śrī Rādhikā is causing the lightening.**

**The paths became slippery due to ecstatic love as They walked in a crooked way. The land of Vṛndāvana was mixed with red paste, sandalwood paste and musk, and thus the ground became muddy.**

**The ocean of love expanded in all directions and Narottama dāsa has drowned in that ocean, not knowing how to swim.**

## Song 53—govinda gopīnātha! kṛpā kari rākha nijapade

*govinda gopīnātha! kṛpā kari rākha nijapade*  
*kāma krodha chayajane laye phire nānāsthāne*  
*viṣaya bhuñjāya nānāmate*  
*haiyā māyāra dāsa kari nānā abhilāṣa*  
*tomārā smaraṇa gela dūre*  
*artha-lābha ei āśe kapāṭa vaiṣṇava beśe*  
*bhramiye beḍai ghare ghare*  
*aneka duḥkhera pare layechile vrajapure*  
*kṛpāḍore galāya bāṅdhiyā*  
*daiva-māyā balātkāre khasaiyā sei ḍore*  
*bhavakūpe dileka ḍāriyā*  
*punaḥ yadi kṛpā kari e-janāra keśe dhari*  
*ṭaniyā tulaha vrajadhāme*  
*tabe se dekhire bhāla natubā parāṇa gela*  
*kahe dīna dāsa narottame*

**Пословный перевод:**

*govinda—*Lord Govinda; *gopīnātha—*the beloved Lord of the gopīs; *kṛpā kari—*please be merciful; *rākha*—please keep; *nijapade—*at Your lotus feet; *kāma*—lust; *krodha—*anger; *chayajane—*six enemies; *laye—*taking; *phire—*wander; *nānāsthāne—*in various places; *viṣaya—*sense objects*; bhuñjāya—*make me enjoy; *nānāmate—*in various ways; *haiya—*being; *māyāra dāsa—*a servant of māyā; *kari—*I do; *nānā*—ṁany; *abhilāṣa—*desires; *tomārā—*Your; *smaraṇa*—remembrance; *gela dūre—*lost; *artha-lābha*—in order to gain wealth; *ei āśe—*with this desire; *kapāṭa—*pseudo; *vaiṣṇava beśe—*in the guise of a Vaiṣṇava; *bhramiye bedāi—*wander; *ghare ghare—*from house to house; *aneka—*so much; *duḥkhera—*of misery; *pare—*after; *layechile—*took me; *vrajapure*—in Vraja-pura; *kṛpāḍore—*with the rope of mercy; *galāya—*in the neck; *bāṅdhiyā—*by tying; *daiva-māyā—*by the illusory energy of the Lord; *balātkāre—*being forced; *khasaiyā—*untie; *sei ḍore—*that rope; *bhavakūpe*—in the well of material existence; *dileka ḍāriyā—*put me in; *punaḥ—*again; *yadi—*if; *kṛpā kari—*please be merciful; *e-janāra—*of this person; *keśe dhari—*pulling the hair; *ṭaniyā tulaha—*pick me up; *vrajadhāme—*in the abode of Vraja; *tabe—*then; *se—*that; *dekhire bhāla—*my only hope; *natuba—*otherwise; *parāṇa gela—*I will die; *kahe—*says; *dīna—*poor; *dāsa narottame—*Narottama dāsa.

**Перевод:**

**O Govinda! The beloved of the gopīs, please keep me at Your lotus feet. The six enemies such as lust, anger drag me to many places and make me enjoy sense objects in various ways.**

**Being a servant of Māyā, I am full of material desires, hence I lost Your remembrance. In order to gain wealth, I wander from door to door in the garb of a pseudo renunciate.**

**After a great deal of distress, You mercifully took me to Vraja. But Your illusory energy forcibly untied the rope of compassion and again placed me in the well of material existence.**

**Please again be merciful to this fallen person and pick me up to Your abode of Vraja by pulling my hair. This is the only hope of my life. Otherwise I am ruined. Thus prays Narottama dāsa.**

## Song 54—mora prabhu madana gopāla

*mora prabhu madana gopāla*  
*śrī govinda gopīnātha tumi anāthera nātha*  
*dayā kara muñi adhamera*  
*saṁsāra sāgara ghore paḍiyāchi kārāgare*  
*kṛpā ḍore bāndhi laha more*  
*adhama caṇḍāla āmi dayāra ṭhākura tumi*  
*śuniyāchi vaiṣṇavera mukhe*  
*e baḍa bharasā mane laye phela vṛndāvane*  
*vaṁśīvaṭa jena dekhi sukhe*  
*kṛpā kara āgu guri laha more keśe dhari*  
*śrī yamunā deha pada-chāyā*  
*aneka dinera āśa nahe jena nairāśa*  
*dayā kara nā kariha māyā*  
*anitya śarīra dhari āpana āpana kari*  
*pāche pāche śamanera bhaya*  
*narottama dāse bhane prāṇa kānde rātri dine*  
*pāche vraja prāpti nāhi haya*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mora*—my; *prabhu—*the Lord; *madana gopāla—*Śrī Madana Gopāla; *śrī govinda gopīnātha—*Śrī Govinda, the Lord of the gopīs; *tumi—*You are; *anāthera nātha—*the protector of the helpless; *dayā kara—*please be merciful; *muñi*—to me; *adhamera—*fallen soul; *saṁsāra—*material existence; *sāgara—*ocean; *ghore—*absorbed; *paḍiyāchi—*I have fallen; *kārāgāre—*in the prison house; *kṛpā ḍore bandhi—*with the rope of mercy; *bāndhi—*bind; *laha more—*please take me; *adhama—*fallen; *caṇḍāla—*dog-eater; *āmi—*I am; *dayāra ṭhākura*—personification of mercy; *tumi—*You are; *śuniyāchi—*I have heard; *vaiṣṇavera mukhe—*from the lips of the Vaiṣṇavas; *e baḍa bharasā*—this is a great hope; *mane*—in my mind; *laye—*pick me up; *phela—*and place me; *vṛndāvane—*in Vṛndāvana; *vaṁśīvaṭa—*the place of vaṁśīvata; *jena—*so that; *dekhi—*I may see; *sukhe—*happily; *kṛpā kara—*be merciful; *āgu guri—*take the initiative; *laha more—*please take me; *keśe dhari*—by pulling my hair; *śrī-yamunā—*Yamunā; *deha pada-chāyā—*give me the shade of Your lotus feet; *aneka dinera—*for a long time; *āśa—*desire; *nahe jena—*may not be; *nairāśa—*disappointed; *dayā kara—*be merciful; *nā kariha māyā—*do not deceive me; *anitya—*temporary; *śarīra—*body; *dhari—*accepting; *āpana āpana kari—*consider it as mine; *pāche pāche—*in the behind; *śamanera bhaya—*the fear of death; *narottama dāse bhane*—Narottama dāsa says; *prāṇa—*life air; *kānde—*cries; *rātri dine—*day and night; *pāche—*so that; *vraja prāpti—*attainment of Vraja; *nāhi haya—*may not happen.

**Перевод:**

**O my Lord Madana Gopāla! O Govinda, the Lord of the gopīs! You are the protector of the helpless. Please be merciful to this fallen soul. I am imprisoned in this formidable ocean of material existence. Kindly pick me up with the rope of Your mercy.**

**I am like a wretched dog-eater, but I have heard from the lips of the saintly Vaiṣṇavas that You are the personification of compassion. O Lord, this is my only hope, therefore please give me shelter in Vṛndāvana so that I may happily see Vaṁśivata.**

**Please come forward and pulling my hair, give me shelter on the banks of the River Yamunā. This is my long cherished desire. Please be merciful to me and do not disappoint me.**

**I consider this temporary material body to be the self and I am so fearful of death. Narottama dāsa says, “My soul is crying day and night with the fear that I may not attain the shelter of Vraja.”**

## Song 55—hari balaba āra madana-mohana heraba go

*hari balaba āra madana-mohana heraba go*  
*ei rūpe vrajera pathe calaba go*  
*jaba go vrajendra-pura ha’ba gopīkāra nupura*  
*tāṅdera caraṇe madhura madhura bājava go*  
*vipine vinoda khelā saṅgete rākhālera melā*  
*tāṅdera caraṇera dhulā mākhaba go*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇera rūpa-mādhurī heraba du’nayana bhari*  
*nikuñjera dvāre dvārī rahiba go*  
*tomarā saba vrajavasī purā-o manera abhilāṣa-e*  
*kabe śrī kṛṣṇera baṅśī śunaba go*  
*ei deha antima kāle rākhaba śrī-jamunāra jale*  
*jaya rādhā govinda bale bhāsaba go*  
*kahe narottama dāsa nā purila abhilāṣa*  
*āra kabe vraje vāsa kareba go*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari*—O Lord Hari; *balaba—*will chant; *āra—*and; *madana-mohana—*the attractor of Cupid; *heraba go—*I will see; *ei rūpe—*in this way; *vrajera pathe—*on the way to Vṛndāvana; *calaba go—*I will walk; *jaba go—*I will go; *vrajendra-pura—*the city of the King of Vraja; *ha’ba—*I will become; *gopīkāra—*of the gopīs; *nupura—*the ankle bells; *tāṅdera—*their; *caraṇe—*on the feet; *madhura madhura—*sweetly; *bājava go—*tinkling sound; *vipine—*in the forest; *vinoda khelā—*the loving pastimes; *saṅgete—*in the association; *rākhālera melā—*innumerable cowherd boys; *tāṅdera—*their; *caraṇera dhulā—*the dust from the feet; *mākhaba go—*I will smear; *rādhā-kṛṣṇera—*of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *rūpa-mādhurī—*the sweet beauty; *heraba—*I will see; *du—*two; *nayana bhari—*to my eyes’ satisfaction; *nikuñjera—*of the groves; *dvāre—*near the gates; *dvārī—*a gatekeeper; *rahiba go—*I will remain; *tomarā saba—*all of you; *vrajavāsī—*residents of Vṛndāvana; *purā-o—*fulfill; *manera—*of the mind; *abhilāṣa-e—*only desire; *kabe*—when will; *śrī kṛṣṇera*—of Lord Kṛṣṇa; *baṅśī—*the flute; *śunaba go—*I will hear; *ei deha—*this body; *antima kāle—*at the time of death; *rākhaba—*will place; *śrī-jamunāra—*of the River Yamunā; *jale—*in the water; *jaya rādhā govinda—*all glories to Rādhā Govinda; *bale—*while chanting; *bhāsaba go—*will float; *kahe—*says; *narottama dāsa—*Narottama dāsa; *nā purila—*did not fulfill; *abhilāṣa—*desires; *āra kabe—*when will; *vraje—*in Vraja; *vāsa karaba go—*will reside.

**Перевод:**

**When will I chant the names of Hari and see Lord Madana Mohana? In this way I want to travel on the path of Vraja.**

**Entering Vraja, Iwill become an ankle-bell on the feet of the gopīs and cause a sweet tinkling sound. I will watch the sporting pastimes of the cowherd boys in the forest of Vṛndāvana and smear the dust from their feet on my body.**

**I will see the sweet beauty of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa to my eyes’ satisfaction and I will stand as a guard at the gate of the groves of Vraja. O residents of Vraja! Please fulfill my desires and allow me to hear the sweet sound of Kṛṣṇa’s flute.**

**At the time of giving up my body, I will simply place it in the waters of the Yamunā and float while chanting, “Jaya Rādhā Govinda.” Narottama dāsa laments thus, “My desires are not fulfilled. When will I reside in Vraja?”**

## Song 56—hari hari! kata dine hena daśā haba

*hari hari! kata dine hena daśā haba*  
*śrī- rāsa-mañjarī saṅge śrī-maṇi-mañjarī rāṅge*  
*śrī-rūpera anugā haiba*  
*suśītala vṛndāvana ratnavedī suśobhana*  
*tāhe maṇimaya siṁhāsana*  
*hema nīla-kāntidhara rāi-kānu sundara*  
*tāhe basāiba duijana*  
*sakhīra ādeśa habe cāmara ḍhulāba kabe*  
*tāmbula yogāba cāṅda-mukhe*  
*ānandita ha’ba sadā śuddha-bhāve prema-kathā*  
*duṅhāra pīriti rasa-sukhe*  
*mallikā mālatī yūthī nānā phule mālā gāṅthi*  
*parāiba duṅhāra galāye*  
*rasera ālasa kāle basiyā caraṇa tale*  
*sevana kariba duṅhāra pāye*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa prāṇapati jīvane maraṇe gati*  
*ihā bine āra nāhi mane*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya-prāṇa svarūpa-rūpa-sanātana*  
*narottama ei nivedana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari hari*!—O Lord Hari; *kata dine—*after how long; *hena—*such; *daśā haba—*my state will be; *śrī-rasa-mañjarī saṅge—*in the association of Śrī Rasa Mañjarī; *śrī-maṇi-mañjarī range—*in the ecstasy of Maṇi-mañjarī; *śrī-rūpera—*of Śrī Rūpa-mañjarī; *anugā—*a follower; *haiba—*will become; *suśītala—*soothing; *vṛndāvana—*Vṛndāvana; *ratnavedī—*on the jewelled altar; *suśobhana—*beautiful; *tāhe—*in that; *maṇimaya—*jewelled; *siṁhāsana—*the throne; *hema—*golden; *nīla—*blue; *kāntidhara—*possessing the complexion of; *rāi—*Śrī Rādhikā; *kānu—*Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *sundara—*enchanting; *tāhe—*on that throne; *basāiba—*make Them sit; *duijana—*both; *sakhīra—*of the sakhīs; *ādeśa habe*—will instruct; *cāmara—*the whisk; *ḍhulāba—*will swing; *kabe*—when; *tāmbūla—*betel nuts; *yogāba*—will supply; *caṇda-mukhe—*in the lotus mouth; *ānandita—*jubilant; *ha’ba sadā—*always become; *śuddha-bhāve—*purely; *prema-kathā—*loving topics; *duṅhāra*—of both; *pirīti rasa—*of the loving mellows; *sukhe—*in ecstasy; *mallikā mālatī yūthī—*mallikā, mālatī, and yūthī flowers; *nānā phule—*various other flowers; *mālā gāṅthi—*make a garland; *parāiba—*I will put; *duṅhāra galāye—*on Their necks; *rasera—*of the pastimes; *ālasa kāle—*during the break; *basiyā—*sitting; *caraṇa tale—*under Their lotus feet; *sevana kariba—*I will serve; *duṅhāra pāye—*massage the feet; *rādhā kṛṣṇa—*Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa; *prāṇapati—*Lord of my heart; *jīvane maraṇe—*in life or death; *gati—*only destination; *ihā bine*—without this; *āra nāhi—*nothing else; *mane—*in my mind; *śrī-kṛṣṇa caitanya prāṇa—*Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya is my life; *svarūpa rūpa sanātana—*Śrīla Śvārūpa, Rūpa, and Sanātana Gosvāmīs; *narottama—*Narottama dāsa; *ei—*only this; *nivedana—*requests.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Hari! How many days more before this will happen to me? In the ecstatic association of Śrī Rasa-mañjarī and Śrī Maṇi-mañjarī, I will become a follower of Śrī Rūpa-mañjarī.**

**In the soothing atmosphere of Vṛndāvana, there is a jewelled throne placed on a jewelled altar and golden-complexioned Rādhā and blue-complexioned Kṛṣṇa sit on that throne.**

**I will swing a camara under the instruction of the gopīs and supply betelnuts to Their lotus mouths. I will always be ecstatic to hear the pure loving topics of Their conjugal pastimes.**

**I will make garlands of various flowers such as māllikā, mālatī, and yūthī, and put them on Their necks and then massage Their lotus feet.**

**Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are my life and soul and the goal of my life. I have no other desire than this. O Lord Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, O Śvarūpa, O Rūpa, O Sanātana, O my lords, this is the only request of Narottama dāsa!**

## Song 57—hede he! nāgara bara śuna he mūralīdhara

*hede he! nāgara bara śuna he mūralīdhara*  
*nivedana kari tuyā pāya*  
*caraṇa nakhara maṇi yena cāṅdera gāṅthani*  
*bhāla śobhe āmāra galāya*  
*śrīdāma sudāma saṅge yakhana vane yāo raṅge*  
*takhana āmi duyāre dāṅḍāye*  
*mane kari saṅge yāi gurūjanara bhaya pāi*  
*āṅkhi raila tuyā pāne ceye*  
*cāi navīna-megha-pāne tuyā baṅdhu paḍe mane*  
*elāiyā keśa nāhi bāṅdhi*  
*randhana-śālāte jāi tuyā baṅdhu guṇa gāi*  
*dhuṅyāra chalanā kari kāṅdi*  
*maṇi nao māṇika nao āṅcale bāṅdhile rao*  
*phula nao ye keśe karibeśe*  
*nārī nā karita vidhi tuyā hena guṇanidhi*  
*laiyā phiritāma deśe deśe*  
*aguru candana haitāma tuyā ange mākhā raitāma*  
*ghāmiyā paḍitāma rāṅgā pāya*  
*ki mora manera sādha vāmana haye cāṅda hāta*  
*vidhi ki sādha pūrābe āmāra*  
*narottama dāsa kaya śuna ahe dayāmaya*  
*tumī āmāya nā chāḍiha dayā*  
*je dina tomāra bhāve āmāra e deha jābe*  
*sei dine diya pada chāyā*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hede he*—O hello; *nāgara bara—*the best of the heroes; *śuna he*—please hear; *mūralīdhara—*Śrī Kṛṣṇa who holds a flute; *nivedana—*request; *kari—*let me do; *tuyā pāya—*at Your lotus feet; *caraṇa nakhara—*the nails of Your feet; *maṇi—*jewel; *yena—*as if; *cāṅdera gāṅthani—*the rows of moon; *bhāla—*beautifully; *śobhe*—decorated; *āmāra galāya—*on my neck; *śrīdāma—*Śrīdāma; *sudāma—*Sudāma *saṅge—*along with; *yakhana—*when; *vane—*in the forest; *yāo raṅge—*go happily; *takhana*—at that time; *āmi—*I am; *duyāre—*at the doorstep; *dāṅḍāye—*stand; *mane kari—*I think; *saṅge yāi*—I go with you; *guru-janara—*of the elderly respectable persons; *bhaya pāi—*be afraid of; *āṅkhi—*the eyes; *raila—*remain fixed; *tuyā pāne ceye*—looking at You; *cāi—*look; *navīna—*new; *megha pāne—*towards the clouds; *tuyā—*You; *bandhu—*O my friends; *paḍe mane—*remember; *elāiyā—*let loose; *keśa—*hair; *nāhi bāṅdhi—*do not comb; *randhana śālāte—*in the kitchen; *jāi—*when I go; *tuyā—*Your; *baṅdhu*—O friend; *guṇa gāi—*sing Your glories; *dhuṅyāra chalanā kari—*on the pretext of smoke; *kāṅdi*—I cry; *maṇi nao—*You are not a jewel; *māṇika nao*—You are not a diamond; *āṅcale—*at the end of a sari; *bāṅdhile—*if I tie; *rao—*You will remain; *phula nao—*You are not a flower; *ye—*that; *keśe*—in the hair; *karibeśe—*decorate; *nārī—*woman; *nā karita—*did not create; *bidhi—*the creator; *tuyā—*You; *hena*—such as; *guṇa nidhi—*the ocean of transcendental qualities; *laiyā—*taking; *phiritāma*—wander; *deśe deśe—*country to country; *aguru—*scented powder; *candana—*sandalwood powder; *haitāma*—if I become; *tuyā*—Your; *aṅge*—on the limbs; *mākhā*—smeared; *raitāma—*remain; *ghāmiyā—*with sweat; *paḍitāma—*fall down; *rāngā pāya—*on the lotus feet; *ki—*what; *mora—*mine; *manera—*of the mind; *sādha—*desire; *vāmana haye—*being like a dwarf; *cāṅda hāta—*touching the moon; *vidhi ki—*will the creator; *sādha—*desire; *purābe*—fulfill; *āmāra—*mine; *narottama dāse*—Narottama dāsa; *kaya—*says; *śuna—*please hear; *ahe dayāmaya*—O most merciful one; *tumī—*You; *āmāya—*to me; *nā chāḍiha*—do not withdraw; *dayā—*compassion; *jedina—*the day; *tomāra*—of You; *bhave—*in the mood; *āmāra*—mine; *e deha*—this body; *jābe—*will perish; *sei dine—*on that day; *diya*—please give; *pada chāyā—*the shelter of Your lotus feet.

**Перевод:**

**O my dear Hero! O Lord, the flute player! Please hear my request to You. Your jewel-like toenails resemble the rows of moons when You go to the forest with Śrīdāma and Sudāma.**

**At that time, I stand at the doorstep thinking of going with You. But I am afraid of the elders and the respectable people. Therefore, my eyes simply gaze after You.**

**Whenever I see the new clouds, O my friend, I immediately remember You and I let loose my hair. When I cook in the kitchen, I simply sing Your glories and cry on the pretext of smoke.**

**O beloved Lord! You are neither a jewel nor a diamond that I can keep You tied to the end of my cloth. You are not a flower that I can decorate my hair with. If I were not a woman, I could have taken You, the ocean of divine qualities, all over the country.**

**If I were aguru or sandalwood, then I would be smeared on Your limbs, and when You would perspire, I would have fallen at Your reddish lotus feet. What desires I have! Although I am a dwarf, still I want to touch the moon. Will the creator ever fulfill my desire?**

**Narottama dāsa says, “Please hear, O most merciful Lord! Please do not neglect me. The day I give up my body being fully absorbed in Your thoughts, please give me shelter at Your lotus feet.”**
